### AG8b — Sheet walk-through: M3 voice · M5 team answers · M6 document audience

Read-only. No repo file touched; scratch (stripped sheet copies + 2 node scripts) lived only under `scratchpad/AG8b/` and is deleted. Nothing built or run.

**Path legend:** `W` = `clinqetwebpartnerapp/src` · `P` = `clinqetmobilepartnerapp/src` · `S` = `clinqetinfrastructure/Services/BusinessSearch` · `L` = `clinqetinfrastructure/Resources/Localization/{en,es,fr,gu,hi}.json` · `WL` = `clinqetwebpartnerapp/public/lang/{en-US,es-US,fr-CA,gu-IN,hi-IN}.json` · `PL` = `clinqetmobilepartnerapp/src/Locales/{en,es,fr,gu,hi}.json`.

**Key-presence + technical-word scan (script, all 5 files each):** WEB 138 keys (`Dictation.*`, `BusinessSearch.*`, `knowledge.audience.*`), MOBILE 147 keys (`dictation.*`, `BUSINESS_SEARCH.*`, `KNOWLEDGE.AUDIENCE.*`), SERVER 74 keys (`BusinessSearch_*`): **0 missing, 0 extra in every language.** Banned-word regex (passage/chunk/index/embedding/retrieval/token/payload/endpoint/stream/cache/blob/SAS/schema/frame/flag/marker/partition/vector + M5's record/query/filter/scope/permission/tool + gu/hi transliterations): **no hit** — the only matches were gu/hi "રેકોર્ડિંગ/रिकॉर्डिंग" (= *recording*, legitimate) and gu "ઍક્સેસ" (= *access*, the sheet's own word). So the "technical word?" column below is **none** for every row unless stated.

---

#### STATES THE CODE HAS THAT NO SHEET DRAWS

- **Dictation disabled server-side** (`AppConfigController.cs:66-72` `Enabled`; `SpeechController.cs:71-73` → 400 `Error_SpeechToTextDisabled`): both clients ignore `enabled` (`AppConfigContext.jsx:127` exposed, `AskBox.jsx:46-56` never reads it; `appConfigService.ts:117` mapped, zero readers) → mic rendered, member records, then "Voice isn't available… Try again" forever.
- **Correction refused** (`SpeechController.cs:104-112`, ForceLocale outside `AllowedLocales` → 400) → surfaces as "Voice unavailable"; unreachable unless config drifts.
- Mobile: any recorder failure rendered as "blocked" (`useSpeechToText.ts:157-160`); language chips visible in the Empty state (`SpeechToTextButton.tsx:256`).
- Server: Team-scope ScopeNote/empty (`WorkToolBase.cs:252-253,271-272`), `DateAmbiguous/Unreadable/TooWide`, `BadStatus`, `FeatureOff`/"NOT SET UP" (`Agent.cs:505-513`), `TooManyMatchesUnknownTotal`, `PartialSearch`, `rowsShortenedForLength` (`WorkToolBase.cs:452-469`), the `invoice_totals` card (Invoice kind, no recordId → "Open invoices").
- M6: whole-box ghost while access unknown (web) / box absent (mobile); read-only + roles-failed → perpetual "Loading the roles…" (both, latent).

---

#### Defects (severity · class · root fix)

1. **Medium · truthfulness/copy** — refusal sentence `L BusinessSearch_NoAccessToTopic` (en:3324; es/fr/gu/hi :3157) says "…Ask the business owner if you need it." and never names the topic; M5 §03 forbids that clause and draws the topic. Fix: reword ×5 (drop the clause); if the topic must be named, format the withheld topic into the sentence at `Agent.cs:490-498`.
2. **Medium · dead control** — `dictation.enabled` read by nobody on either app (`AskBox.jsx:46-56`; `SpeechToTextButton.tsx:91-105`). Fix: don't mount the mic when `enabled === false`, the same rule as `isSupported`.
3. **Medium · parity/dead control** — mobile "Try again" on TooLong (`useSpeechToText.ts:280-282`, `SpeechToTextButton.tsx:268`) re-sends a clip the server already refused. Fix: `canRetry` = Failed only, as web (`DictationMic.jsx:140`).
4. **Medium · delivered cap unread** — mobile never passes `dictation.maxAudioBytes` to the hook (`SpeechToTextButton.tsx:105`), so an oversize clip is refused by 413 after speaking — the outcome web already fixed (`AskBox.jsx:50-52`). Fix: thread `maxAudioBytes` into `useSpeechToText` and check the file size in `stopRecording` before `transcribe()`.
5. **Medium · parity/data loss** — mobile audience picker has no `lastPicked` (`index.tsx:1995`): Everyone → Only certain roles lands on owner-and-admins, two taps lose the roles with no undo. Fix: a `lastPicked` ref seeded from the doc, passed on the Roles radio (web Box :70,77,108,248).
6. **Low · parity/one action** — Review card: web deep-links `?reviewId=` (`BusinessSearchPage.jsx:266-268`), mobile lands on the Reviews list (`index.tsx:255`). Fix: navigate with `{ reviewId }` and highlight on the Reviews screen.
7. **Low · unregistered sheet deviation** — Customer/Call/Refund actions open LISTS on both apps (page :255,:284,:289; screen :238,:270,:275) while M5 §06 says "opens the record"; no per-record routes exist. Fix: a PLAN §0.1 ruling (as R1) or highlight params those pages actually read.
8. **Low · unregistered sheet deviation** — the screen's "Showing 10 of 47 · Open Refund requests" line is not built (no key; wire carries no total/shown; `askRules.js:221-223`). Fix: ruling, or carry total/shown on the Done frame + key ×5×2.
9. **Low · unregistered sheet deviation** — chip ORDER (`askRules.js:335-344` work chips before the second service name; sheet §10 puts service names first). R1 covers which chips, not order. Fix: PLAN §0.1 row or reorder.
10. **Low · copy** — web roles-failed renders "The roles could not be loaded. Try again. Try again" (`TeamSearchAudienceBox.jsx:277-281` + `WL` 5838, whose text already ends "Try again." in all 5). Fix: web needs a sentence without the trailing "Try again." (mobile uses the full sentence as its tap target, so split the keys).
11. **Low · rendering rule/brand** — mobile near-cap is red not amber (`SpeechToTextButton.tsx:320,328`) and the recording state is red-tinted (:233,:307) vs web amber near-cap (`DictationMic.jsx:89,126`) and brand-green pressed (:62). Fix: amber for nearCap, `#97EF29` for recording.
12. **Low · undrawn copy on both apps** — "Wrapping up in N seconds", "Listening again in {language}…", "Type your question instead…", "Not right?" have no key; only tone changes. Fix: keys ×5×2 rendered per state, or a ruling that colour alone is the state.
13. **Low · latent** — read-only box + roles failed → "Loading the roles…" forever (Box :194-201; screen :1952); unreachable today.
14. **Low · hygiene** — orphan `KNOWLEDGE.AUDIENCE.NONE_PICKED` ×5 mobile files. Fix: delete.
15. **Low · a11y** — web row mark ≈21px hit target (`KnowledgePage.jsx:278`); mobile mark label is the action only (`index.tsx:1387`) vs web's setting-then-action (:274-277).
16. **Info · client constants** — `cap-10` amber window (W :57,110 / P :109); `maxLength={500}` on both (mirrors `MaxLength(500)` on the DTO, not delivered); `LOCALE_LABELS`/`LABELS` 13-entry map with raw-code fallback (W :41-46 / P `DictationLanguageChips.tsx:29-36`) — a server candidate outside it prints "pa-IN".
17. **Info** — M5 §04 "two people with the same first name → ask which one": no prompt rule (`Agent.cs:628-635` handles "nobody" only); behaviour unverified.
18. **Info** — M5 §11 "decided fresh on every question": IsSolo and the roster are cached for `RosterCacheSeconds` (`RosterService.cs:57-61,96-100`).

#### Money-contract findings

1. Symbol table identical ×3 (backend `clinqetshared/Helpers/CurrencyMinorUnit.cs:26-47`, web `src/utils/currencyMinorUnit.js:11-32`, phone `src/Util/currencyMinorUnit.ts:31-52`; 20 codes incl. SEK/NOK "kr", JPY/CNY "¥"); pinned by `Clinqet.API.UnitTests/Services/CurrencyMinorUnitTests.cs:188-200` with `Assert.SkipWhen` (§0.17 compliant). Exponent list JPY+KRW on all three (`.cs:18`, `.js:9`, `.ts:29`; pinned `:30-42`, `:135-185`).
2. Locale per language: backend `clinqetcore/Utilities/LanguageCulture.cs:26-33` = phone `MONEY_LOCALE` `.ts:7-13` (en-US/es-US/fr-CA/gu-IN/hi-IN); phone money and counts both use `productLocale` (`.ts:86`, `src/Util/formatNumber.ts:14`), never `getActiveLocale` ✓. WEB passes the BARE language to `IntlProvider` (`src/app/context/IntlContext.jsx:14-19,44-47`). Measured (node 24, ICU 78): en/hi/gu equal their product tags (hi/gu group 2,08,511.10 ✓ Indian grouping), but `fr` groups with U+202F where `fr-CA` groups with U+00A0 → web French money is not byte-identical to phone/backend (the `.cs:64-65` "30/30" claim assumes product tags). Spanish is not wired on provider web (`IntlContext.jsx:14-19`; `src/components/common/SmartLnDropdown.jsx:22-28`) though `es-US.json` carries every M8 key.
3. Empty code ⇒ "" and unknown ⇒ "$" on both twins (`.js:44-47`, `.ts:65-68`); backend `Symbol` default "$" (`.cs:46`), `Parse` → USD vs `TryParse` no fallback (`.cs:51-59`). `formatMajor` `maximumFractionDigits` overrides digits only (`.js:55-64`, `.ts:76-94`; used by `WeeklyChart.jsx:47`).
4. Currency NAME keys: 20/20 in all five catalogues, both apps (`en-US.json:6096-6115`, counts es/fr/gu/hi = 20; `en.json:2613-2634`, 20 in each) — NOK = "Norwegian kroner"/"coronas noruegas"/"couronnes norvégiennes"/"નોર્વેજિયન ક્રોના"/"नॉर्वेजियन क्रोना"; pinned `src/utils/currencyName.test.js:39-57`, `__tests__/currencyName.test.ts:22-33`.
5. Headline = business currency, server-resolved on every surface (`DashboardController.cs:71`, `InvoiceController.cs:782`, `InvoiceService.cs:359` → `Services/Currency/CurrencyService.cs:39-44`, default USD `:51`); every client reads `data.currency` first (`DashboardActivity.jsx:55`, `YourEarnings.jsx:39`, `InvoiceSummaryCards.jsx:79`, `index.tsx:433`, `YourEarnings.tsx:184`, `InvoiceSummaryCards.tsx:66`); Redux/Context code is the pre-arrival guess and is "" for the four `insights.read`-less roles (`src/components/common/CurrencyInitializer.jsx:41-42`; `src/context/CurrencyProvider.tsx:44,78-79`). Handled without inventing "$" everywhere EXCEPT the phone group heading — `InvoicesScreen/index.tsx:694` passes `currencyCode` (not the screen's own `summaryCurrency` `:323`), so `invoiceDue.ts:133-135` promotes the first row's currency: a technician on an INR business whose first due row is CAD reads "Due this week C$24,999.00 / Plus ₹78,999.00 in Indian rupees".
6. Zero business-currency rows in a group: backend headline 0 + others listed on all three server surfaces (tests above); phone heading with a known primary → "₹0.00" + Plus line — consistent with the sheet's rule, undrawn.
7. Partition scope: all four reads pass `businessId` (`clinqetinfrastructure/Data/COSMOS/InvoiceRepository.cs:225,256,323,361` → `Data/COSMOS/Base/CosmosDbRepository.cs:407-418,528-540` `PartitionKey = new PartitionKey(partitionKey)`); container key `/businessId` (`clinqetcore/Cosmos/Setup/CosmosContainerPolicies.cs:220`). Three are GROUP BY after the WHERE (`:222` status+currency, `:251`, `:318`); the range read (`:355`) returns rows grouped in-service (`InvoiceService.cs:611-637`, bounded 90 days `InvoiceController.cs:41`). `/currency/?` is not an included path (`CosmosContainerPolicies.cs:222-253`) and needs none; memory `money-one-definition-p46-2026-09-05.md:31-35` records +0.15/+0.10/+0.02 RU measured. No index change ✓.
8. `count` vs `primaryCurrencyCount`: both emitted (`Cosmos.cs:2958-2964`) and consumed by every reader — `Services/BusinessSearch/Tools/InvoiceTotalsTool.cs:118-119,129-136` (also top-level `grandTotal`/`otherCurrencies`), web `Invoices.jsx:170-182` + `InvoiceSummaryCards.jsx:158,187`, phone `InvoiceSummaryCards.tsx:92,109,114`. No other consumer (MCP/functions: none). Web recomputes a client-side grand total over headline amounts only (`InvoiceSummaryCards.jsx:107`) — share bars exclude foreign money; a foreign-only status shows "0 % of total billed" beside a non-empty list.

Earnings entries carry `count 0` (`DashboardService.cs:41`, `InvoiceService.cs:637`) so `CurrencyTotals.cs:41` drops a 0.00 foreign payment from tile/chart lists but keeps it on cards; `dashboardAlsoPaidToday.test.tsx:94-97` pins a "C$0.00" entry the wire cannot produce.

Test coverage gaps: integration tests call services directly with `CurrencyCode.INR` (`ProviderMoneyMultiCurrencyIntegrationTests.cs:58,103`) — no HTTP-level proof of controller currency resolution; the overdue per-currency carve-out (4th query) is unit-only (`InvoiceServiceTests.cs:1012-1034`), the integration summary seeds Paid rows only (`:125-127`); no phone tile loading/failure test; no empty-primary heading test.

## 13. Live-data facts (dev, both regions) and the checks still owed

### 13.1 Method
A node probe library in the session scratchpad (deleted at the end of the session; nothing under any repo) read the Cosmos key, the AI Search key and the Storage key AT RUNTIME from `C:\Nik\cosmosindexsetup\appsettings.ca.json` / `.in.json` per call — no value was written to a file or printed. Cosmos via the REST API (HMAC master-key auth; cross-partition only for probe counts, partition-scoped for every RU measurement so the numbers are the production shape), AI Search via the REST search/facet API, Storage via SharedKey blob listing. Cosmos and SQL returned 403/"IP not allowed" for `74.12.4.237` on 2026-09-05; the owner allow-listed the IP on 2026-09-06 and the Cosmos checks below ran then. **SQL was not probed** (no SQL helper in the probe; the promo/connected-account checks in §8 remain code-only).

### 13.2 What the regions hold (2026-09-06)

### 13.3 What these facts change in the findings
- SKILL §19.6 / AUDIT-P4.7 §9 / AG4 / AG7 / AG11 "`/approvalStatus/?` not applied to any stamp" → STALE; the owner already ran `cosmosindexsetup`; the leg costs ~3.7 RU (measured), the "36 RU / ~3 extrapolated" sentences become "3.7 measured".
- AG9-F1 (`MinimumHours` lost on the index leg) is UNREACHABLE today (0 hourly services) — a §0.7 candidate the owner can defer with zero live cost.
- M6's "restricted document" states (audience Roles) and M9's "Show page N" state are UNREACHABLE with live data (0 restricted rows; every card page = 1; no `_ocr` cache) — a real-data browser pass cannot show them without seeding.
- The cross-currency inbox items C1-1 (sort across currencies) and C5-1 (provider index envelope) are LIVE for LXDP8G and MEE3IC; C2 (carts) has no live data at all; C5-2 (offers > 100 %) is LIVE for two IN businesses.
- Lane A's storage cut has never fired; the RE-ASK path (A-9) HAS (two sessions).

### 13.4 Still owed (needs the owner or a later session)
- SQL: promo codes with money caps and `Currency == null`; `__EFMigrationsHistory` on both stamps for the two +0530 migrations (§9).
- The S6 shared-search load measurement (PLAN §15c S6) as a NUMBER — 20 concurrent members of one business asking at once; the probe library times a request, the fixing session runs it against dev after B9.
- The 13 SX3SG2 orphan blobs: delete (they belong to no row; ~storage cost only) — owner's call, a one-line blob delete per prefix.
- Live gu/hi model probes (C1, C7) and the stale-citation measurement (A3).



## 14. Localization ×15 (AG3), config hygiene and deployment (AG6), untouched code and failure paths (AG13)

All three finished on 2026-09-06; their defects are folded into §5/§6 (D-1, D-14, D-15, D-16, D-20, U-32…U-38) and §3 (B-7, B-11, B-16, B-19); the reports follow in full for the evidence.


### 14.1 Localization (AG3)

### AG3 — Localization audit ×15 files (Business Search / Ask Clinket programme)

Method: every check scripted (node, read-only) under the session scratchpad `…\scratchpad\AG3\` (`check1_2_6_7.js`, `c2_icu.js`, `c3_refs.js`, `check4_techwords.js`, `check5_translations.js`, `check8_drift.js`, `extract_mockups.js`, plus `lib.js` duplicate-detecting JSON parser). No repo file was created, edited or deleted; no build/test/git-write command was run. Every claim below was re-read against source before reporting.

#### 1. Findings table

**Root fixes.** D1: one noun per language (the `Analytics.Title`/`ANALYTICS.TITLE` value) reused by the 3 Ask Clinket keys and the 2 team-area keys. D2: owner picks one transliteration per script; sweep the 4 gu/hi UI files (counts above); decide whether Latin "Clinket" is allowed in Indic copy. D3: one author per state — the sheets say the server: render `frame.text`/the error body and delete the 3 screen title keys ×2 apps; or, if the screen must own it (the replayed variant), delete the 3 server sentences and correct the sheet rows; delete `BusinessSearch_Status_Working` duplication by falling back to the server sentence. D4/D7: translate once per sentence, fr-CA rule decided once, copy to the twin. D5: delete the 18 UI keys ×5 and the dead `invoiceDue.js` exports; set or delete `ERROR.OFFLINE`. D6(ii)(iii): replace "Ready" with તૈયાર/तैयार, "AI Knowledge" with the page's own gu/hi name. D8: add `_One` siblings where a real count flows (drafts-ready body).

- Observed outside the programme, not counted: backend `Notification_BookingCashRefundRecorded_Body` says **"Clinqet"** in all 5 languages (customer-facing); Identity keys `UserProfileUpdateError` "{{error}}" (renders the literal `{error}` under `string.Format`), `InvalidVerificationCodeWithAttempts {remaining}`, `MfaLockedOut {minutes}` (named placeholders); web `defaultMessage=` inline English duplicates in `components/dashboard/home/*.jsx` (e.g. `DashboardActivity.jsx:120`); es whole-file vosotros forms web 4 / mobile 3.

### 14.2 Config hygiene, deployment, infrastructure (AG6)

### AG6 — CONFIG HYGIENE, DEPLOYMENT & INFRASTRUCTURE (Business Search / Ask Clinket, P1→P4.7)

Read-only. Method: node scripts under scratchpad `AG6\` (`flat.js` = JSON flattener with line numbers; `parity.js` = C# default evaluator vs appsettings per host; `readers.js` = reader + host-reachability scan over all non-test `.cs`), `git log/diff` for the programme window, PowerShell `Parser.ParseFile` on deploy.ps1. Every cited line re-read. No repo file touched; scratch copies of appsettings deleted; `git status --porcelain` in all 12 repos shows nothing from this session.

### 14.3 Untouched dependencies, edge cases, failure paths (AG13)

### AG13 — Untouched dependencies · edge cases · failure paths (Business Search / Ask Clinket)

Read-only. Scope traced once per path across `clinqetapi` (controller), `clinqetinfrastructure` (agent, model client, tools, services), `clinqetwebpartnerapp`, `clinqetmobilepartnerapp`. Known items (AG1-03, AG4-01/11, AG5-D2, AG7-D1, AG8a-D3/D5/D6/D7/D8, AG8c-D2) are referenced, not re-found.

#### 1. Findings

| ID | Sev | Where | Defect (one line) | CLASS |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AG13-01 | **HIGH** | mobile | A 403 carrying `billing_only_access` / `business_context_invalid` / `business_context_required` on the ask stream renders **nothing** — spinner stops, blank under the question | refusal classifier carve-out assumes a layer the hand-rolled stream bypasses |
| AG13-02 | MEDIUM | web + mobile | Expired session (401 + refresh failure) on the ask path never signs out: red "Something went wrong" + Try again, forever | stream path skips the shared 401→logout policy |
| AG13-03 | MEDIUM | server + both apps | A dictated question >500 chars gets "Validation failed. Please check the provided information." — the too-long advice sits in `errors[]`, which neither app reads | validation envelope shape vs client rendering; code comments claim otherwise in 3 places |
| AG13-04 | MEDIUM | mobile | PLAN §16 "continue state" (`suspended`) is unreachable: backgrounding >20 s ends in the red Generic error; both CTAs re-ask and charge a second unit | state cleared by the same settle that should show it |
| AG13-05 | MEDIUM | server | Per-**business** token window (100k/min) refuses with the per-**member** burst sentence "You're asking a little too quickly", no Retry-After, unit already spent, can fire mid-answer | wrong copy for a shared-budget refusal |
| AG13-06 | MEDIUM | server (untouched dep) | Knowledge retrieval is bounded by the phone's `Voice:Knowledge:RetrievalTimeoutMs=4000`; `BusinessSearch:Retrieval:PerLegTimeoutSeconds=20` is dead; >4 s ⇒ C6 "unavailable", never a partial | screen surface governed by a voice dial |
| AG13-07 | MEDIUM | server | `list_team_members` puts an unnamed member's **e-mail address** in the card title, payload `name` and prompt | fallback display name (email) not sanitized in the tool |
| AG13-08 | LOW-MED | server (untouched dep) | SQL roster failure ⇒ no PEOPLE block, schema still offers `assignedToMemberId` ⇒ a colleague question answers unfiltered or "could not tell who you meant" | failure collapsed into "empty roster" at the prompt |
| AG13-09 | LOW-MED | server (untouched dep) | Category cache miss + Cosmos blip fails a **successful** catalogue answer as C6 "unavailable" (breadcrumb only) | cosmetic read on the answer's critical path |
| AG13-10 | LOW-MED | both apps | Image SAS = 30 min; older answer ⇒ broken/blank tile, no placeholder, no sentence | no `onError` state |
| AG13-11 | LOW | both apps | Clean stream end with zero frames and zero words ⇒ nothing rendered (guard only fires when `produced`) | last silent path |
| AG13-12 | LOW | both apps | `dictation.enabled` from AppConfig read by nobody; disabled dictation ⇒ mic shown, 400 rendered as "Voice isn't available just now" + Try again | delivered flag unconsumed |
| AG13-13 | LOW | server | Error frame after deltas carries no `Partial`; half answer stored as complete + full footer shown | terminal frame semantics |
| AG13-14 | LOW | mobile | Stall reported to analytics as `error_0` (web reports `stalled`) | parity |
| AG13-15 | LOW | server | Missing BusinessProfile row ⇒ profile/hours/offers answer "try again in a few minutes" (permanent shown as transient) | null → Unavailable |
| AG13-16 | LOW | both | Unknown citation kind ⇒ footer "From your business details" | forward-compat |
| AG13-17 | LOW | web | `prepare` on page open pops the shared refusal dialog before any question (BillingOnly/revoked) | interceptor before `.catch` |
| AG13-18 | LOW | both | Daily cap: "It resets at midnight UTC." + "Try again in about 1440 min." | Retry-After reused as minutes |
| AG13-19 | LOW | server | Ask #2 while #1 streams: persisted turns can land out of order in the session | two writers, request-lifetime persist |
| AG13-20 | LOW | web | Handed-question slot not cleared on sign-out (30 s window bounds it) | purge list incomplete |
| AG13-21 | LOW | latency | 5 s margin between ping (15 s) and stall (20 s); no per-tool timeout; blob NetworkTimeout 180 s > answer clock 150 s | timing budgets |

#### 2. Details

**AG13-01 (mobile, silent).** `clinqetmobilepartnerapp/src/services/businessSearchService.ts:454` raises the dialog only if `isAccessRefusalBody(result.status, body)`; `src/services/accessDeniedBus.ts:98-108` returns true for `permission_denied` or a **code-less** 403 and explicitly false for `business_context_required` / `business_context_invalid` / `billing_only_access` ("owned by the workspace gate"). The stream never passes `fetchWithAuth`, so `logForbidden` (`src/apiManager/apiManager.tsx:229-232`) never sees it. The hook then does `if (failure.status === 403) { setLocalErrorKey(null); }` (`src/hooks/useBusinessSearch.ts:277-279`). Sequence: business suspended (server `RequiresPermissionAttribute.cs:92-97` → 403 `billing_only_access`) or membership revoked after the 60 s snapshot (`TenantContextMiddleware.cs:292-298` → `NoContext` 403 `RequiresPermissionAttribute.cs:151-169`) → member presses Ask → spinner → stops → screen shows only the "You:" line. Charged? No (refused before `EnforceLimitsAsync`). Web is clean: `accessDeniedBus.js:89-97` treats every 403 except `policy_update_required` as a refusal. ROOT: the stream service must raise (or hand the hook the server's `message`) for **every** 403; the hook must never null the key unless the bus reported it opened the dialog.

**AG13-02 (both, dead session).** Web: `businessSearchService.js:512-518` refreshes once; on `{success:false}` (`tokenRefreshService.js:29-99` never logs out) `result` is still the 401 → `throw` → hook `useBusinessSearch.js:266-271`: 401 body from JwtBearer is empty → `readErrorBody` null → `BusinessSearch.Error.Generic` + Try again. The shared client logs out on the same result (`apiClient.js:151-159`). Mobile: `businessSearchService.ts:493-508` (`refreshTokenAPI` "never throws… redirect is the caller's responsibility", `refreshToken.tsx:82-91`) vs `apiManager.tsx:212-216` `forceLogout()`. Outcome: "Something went wrong. Your question was not lost." — the member retries forever. ROOT: call the platform's forced logout on a non-transient refresh failure from the stream service (mobile `isTransientRefreshFailure` exists at `refreshToken.tsx:46-49`).

**AG13-03 (server + both).** Reachable only by voice: `AskBox.jsx:59-63` sets the field programmatically (bypasses `maxLength={500}` at line 112; mobile `index.tsx:657-660` same) and 60 s of speech is ~700-1000 chars. `BusinessSearchController.cs:102` returns `ValidationFailed()` **before** `ResolveLanguage` (106); `BaseController.cs:777-785` builds `ApiResponse.Fail(localized "Error_ValidationFailed", errors)` → `message` = "Validation failed. Please check the provided information." (`en.json:553`), advice in `errors[]`. Web renders `failure.response.data.message` (`useBusinessSearch.js:270-271`), mobile `body.message` (`businessSearchService.ts:445-447`, `useBusinessSearch.ts:288`). Language: Accept-Language, which on web is `selectedLanguage` (`apiHeaders.js:42-63`, same key as `IntlContext.jsx:22`) and on mobile the stored language (`businessSearchService.ts:430-433`) — consistent in practice, but the comment at controller 103-105 is wrong for this branch. ROOT: length check after `ResolveLanguage`, top-level message = `Error_BusinessSearchQuestionTooLong` (as the empty-question branch 108-110 already does).

**AG13-04 (mobile).** `useBusinessSearch.ts:107-112` sets `suspended` on non-active AppState while running. iOS suspends JS; on return the overdue stall timer fires (`sseStream.ts:697-707`) → reject → hook catch → line 270 `setSuspended(false)` → 280-283 `BusinessSearch.Error.Generic` + `partial`. The calm box `index.tsx:572-583` renders only when `!running`, i.e. after the settle that just cleared it → never visible for the case it was designed for. Member sees red "Something went wrong" for a normal app switch; both "Try again" (596-608) and "Ask again" (575-581) call `submit(asked)` → a new charged ask (server already persisted the partial turn via the client-abort path `BusinessSearchController.cs:181-189`). ROOT: `suspendedRef`; when the run settles with a transport failure after a background transition, show the suspended copy, not Generic; prefer `reopen(sessionId)` over a re-ask.

**AG13-05 (server).** `BusinessSearchAgent.cs:159-181`: `CheckTokenRateLimitAsync("business-search", businessId, 1500)` against `AIAssistant:RateLimiting:BusinessSearch = {WindowMinutes:1, TokensPerWindow:100000}` (appsettings), bucket keyed `business-search:{businessId}` (`AiRateLimitingService.cs:99-131, 235`) — one window for the whole business. `RecordTokenUsageAsync` (232) adds prompt+completion per model turn. Cost per answer: system+rules ~2k, replayed history ≤12 turns × ≤4000 chars ≈ 12k, tool schemas, tool results ≤18 000 chars ≈ 4.5k, × up to 4 iterations → a deep follow-up records ~40-80k. So: single-turn questions ≈ 20-30/min per business; deep follow-ups ≈ 1-2/min. The refusal is `BusinessSearch_Error_TooFast` — a sentence about the member's typing pace — with no Retry-After, after the daily units were spent (AG1-03), and it can fire at iteration 2+ with tools already run (`wroteAnswer` false → Error). ROOT: distinct key ("busy, try in a minute") carrying `retryAfter`; check before spending.

**AG13-06 (untouched dep).** `ProviderKnowledgeSearchService.Provider.cs:62-64` `budgetMs = Voice:Knowledge:RetrievalTimeoutMs` (4000 in appsettings), `deadline.CancelAfter(budgetMs)`; the per-leg clock (187-189) is linked **under** it, so `SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:87` passing `PerLegTimeoutSeconds=20` is inert. Visibility sweep (whole partition, 73) + embedding share + every leg must finish in 4 s; past it → 154-162 → `Unavailable` → agent 298-308 → "Search is unavailable right now. Please try again in a few minutes." A per-leg failure gives a partial-search note (SearchKnowledgeTool 250-260); a budget expiry gives an outright refusal. ROOT: the provider overload takes its own budget from `BusinessSearchSettings.Retrieval`.

**AG13-07 (server).** `ListTeamMembersTool.cs:627-633` `Text(member.DisplayName)`; `MemberLifecycleService.cs:986-990` returns `email` when first/last are empty. Title, payload `name` and the model prompt then carry the address; the tool's own note (663) says never. `BusinessSearchRosterService.cs:294-299` already guards the prompt roster. ROOT: same `SafeName` rule in the tool (id or a localized "Unnamed member").

**AG13-08 (untouched dep, SQL).** `BusinessSearchAgent.cs:616` uses `ListAsync`, which drops `Read=false` (`BusinessSearchRosterService.cs:250-251, 271-279`) → `roster.Count < 2` → no PEOPLE block and no "say you could not tell who they meant" instruction (629-635); `BusinessSearchWorkToolBase.cs:106-113` still offers `assignedToMemberId`. "Gaurav's bookings this week" during a SQL blip → the model passes no id (whole-business rows, no `scopeNote`) or an invented one → `Roster.ResolveAsync` (203-210) → `MemberNotResolved`. Neither sentence says the team list could not be read. `IsSoloAsync` fails safe (233-240). ROOT: `TryListAsync` at the prompt; on `Read=false` an explicit "team list unavailable" block, and `ResolveAsync` → `Unavailable` (C6).

**AG13-09.** `SearchServicesTool.cs:537, 689-691` → `CategoryRepository.GetGlobalCategoriesAsync` → `CategoryCacheService.cs:48-90` (no catch around `fetchCategories()`) → exception → `BusinessSearchToolBase.cs:109-114` → `TemporarilyUnavailable` → C6 error, after the catalogue legs already succeeded. ROOT: breadcrumb lookup best-effort.

**AG13-10.** `StorageConfiguration.ReadSasExpiryMinutes=30` (`AzureStorageService.cs:130`); web `AnswerImages.jsx:552-557` `<img>` without `onError`, lightbox 563-570 with the same URL; mobile `index.tsx:511-514, 728-733` `<Image>` without `onError`. Outcome: broken-image glyph (web) / blank tile (mobile), no sentence.

**AG13-11.** Server path that ends a 200 stream with no terminal frame: `BusinessSearchController.cs:191-199` (`Response.HasStarted` → `EmptyResult`), or a graceful proxy close. Clients: web `useBusinessSearch.js:281` / mobile `useBusinessSearch.ts:297` set `partial` only when `produced`; with zero deltas nothing is set → question line, no answer, no error. Rare but the last silent path.

**AG13-12.** `AppConfigContext.jsx:127` computes `enabled`, no consumer (`AskBox.jsx`, `DictationMic.jsx` never read it); mobile `SpeechToTextButton.tsx:91-105` reads only `maxRecordingSeconds`. Server disabled → `SpeechController.cs:71-73` 400 → `useDictation.js:527-533` / `useSpeechToText.ts:244-246` → "Voice isn't available just now" + Try again (re-sends the same clip, 400 again).

**AG13-13.** `BusinessSearchAgent.cs:298-308` (C6 after the model streamed prose with its tool calls) and 344-361 write `Error` without `Partial`; `BusinessSearchController.cs:167-171, 202` persists `partial:false`; web `BusinessSearchPage.jsx:533-586` shows the full footer + red box (600-614); mobile `index.tsx:496-556, 585-594` same. Stored half-prose is replayed as a finished answer.

**AG13-14.** `sseStream.ts:705` `reject(new Error(...))` — no `stalled`; `useBusinessSearch.ts:259` → `error_0`. Web `businessSearchService.js:475-479` sets it.

**AG13-15.** `FullProviderContextService.cs:159-164` null for an unknown business (auto-created only when the profile page is opened); `GetBusinessProfileTool.cs:53-55`, `GetAvailabilityTool.cs:167-169`, `ListOffersTool.cs:761-763` → `TemporarilyUnavailable`.

**AG13-17.** `businessSearchService.js:532-533` `.catch(() => null)` runs after the interceptor `apiClient.js:63-72` has already raised the dialog (POST ⇒ user-initiated ⇒ not quieted, `accessDeniedBus.js:63-65`). `page.jsx` states the opposite intent. Mobile `postWithUrl` → `logForbidden` → coded 403 → silent (best effort).

**AG13-18.** `BusinessSearchController.cs:397-398, 441` Retry-After = seconds to UTC midnight; web `BusinessSearchPage.jsx:608-612`, mobile `index.tsx:589-591` render `Math.ceil(retryAfterSeconds/60)` minutes after the server's own "resets at midnight UTC" (`en.json:3315-3316`).

**AG13-19.** Ask #1 aborted by #2 (`useBusinessSearch.js:125`) → server persists #1 on `CancellationToken.None` (`BusinessSearchController.cs:188, 465-471`) while #2 reads the session (133) and later appends; CAS in `McpSessionService.cs:368-411` keeps both writes but not their order.

#### 3. Failure-path matrix

| Path | Web (file:line · behaviour) | Mobile (file:line · behaviour) | Server (file:line · behaviour) | Provider sees | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| a. drop mid-stream, no terminal | `businessSearchService.js:474-489` read throws → status 0; hook `:258-265` Offline/Generic + partial if words | `sseStream.ts:755-762` onerror reject; hook `:280-283` Generic + partial | `BusinessSearchController.cs:181-189` OCE → partial persisted | words kept + red box (AG8a-D7); zero words + **clean** EOF ⇒ nothing (AG13-11) | known + AG13-11 |

#### 6. Part 4 — latency

Before the first byte (no pings possible): `EnforceLimitsAsync` 2 point reads + up to 2 PATCH (`:410-434`) + session point read (`:133`); the client stall guard (20 s) is armed at fetch start (`businessSearchService.js:429`, `sseStream.ts:783`). Then, inside the first `MoveNextAsync`: alphabets cache hit or ≤250 ms (`BusinessAlphabetService.cs:61-76`), `IsSoloAsync` + roster (SQL, 60 s cache), message build, Meta, model TTFT — pings from 15 s (`:153-160`). Tool: Status before `ExecuteAsync` (`BusinessSearchAgent.cs:423-429`), then no frames ⇒ pings cover it; **no per-tool timeout** — a 60 s Cosmos work query runs to the 150 s answer clock (`:137-139`), then "took too long"; blob existence checks inside the answer sit under a 180 s storage timeout. Knowledge search is cut at 4 s total (AG13-06); catalogue at 8 s (`Voice:Catalog:LookupTimeoutMs`). Model leg 60 s + 10 s HttpClient (`Program.cs:1053-1057`); 4 legs × 60 > 150 ⇒ the total clock wins → PartialAnswer/TookTooLong (`:318-327`). Ping 15 s vs stall 20 s = 5 s margin (AG13-21).

#### 7. Verified clean (how)

Terminal-frame discipline: every agent exit writes exactly one terminal frame (`:161-181, 249-279, 298-308, 318-343, 344-361, 370-396`) except the client-gone branch (339-343) — read end to end. C6 discipline: `BusinessSearchToolBase.cs:101-114`, `.Provider.cs:95-100, 125-133`, `SearchServicesTool.cs:505-506, 644-650`. Burst/daily caps and the read-then-increment order (`:404-434`). Frame parsing tolerates pings and unknown event types on both apps (`askRules.js:79-104`, `sseStream.ts:643-654`, hooks `default: break`). Localization parity: 79 server keys × 5 languages, 112 web × 5, 109 mobile × 5 — zero missing. Refresh single-flight both apps. Session ownership (`McpSessionService.cs:353, 380-386`). Image SAS minting failure ⇒ card without picture (`SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:326-337`). Governor is a no-op for the Interactive lane (`AiBudgetGovernor.cs:65`). Cutoff alert publish swallows (`AiBudgetCutoffAlerts.cs:146-178`).

#### 8. Not verified

`BusinessAccessPolicy.Classify` (clinqetshared) not read; the detail pages a work citation opens (booking/quote/invoice not-found handling, mobile `InvoiceDetailScreen` resolving an id-shaped `invoiceNumber`); the approved sheet's colour for the "page can't be shown" state (rendered red on both apps, described as normal in code); iOS timer-resume semantics stated from RN behaviour, not executed; server-side `AnalyticsController` Service Bus failure path not read (client swallow verified).

### 15.1 The §0.7 tables the fixing session presents if Q11 is "add"

| Column | `minimumHours` on the services search index |
|---|---|
| What | `ServiceSearchDocument.minimumHours` — `Edm.Double`, retrievable, not filterable/facetable |
| Who reads it | `ProviderCatalogSearchService.ToItem(SearchDocument)` (index leg) → `CatalogPrice.MinimumHours` → `BusinessSearchMoney.Hourly` (screen) and the voice `FormatPriceText` |
| Who writes it | `AzureSearchIndexer` from `Service.Pricing.MinimumHours` (already selected for the Cosmos leg) |
| Why not already stored | the index carries `hourlyRate` but not the minimum the price is conditional on; the Cosmos row has it |
| Why not a constant | per-service value |
| Cost | one nullable double per service doc (~1 KB per 1,000 docs); no re-index needed (additive field; existing docs read null until re-indexed by the change feed) |
| What breaks if omitted | today NOTHING (0 hourly services live); the first hourly service with a minimum answers without its condition on an indexed stamp |

The inbox's `(currency, amount)` composite indexes (C1-1/C1-2) and the `currency` filterable attribute on both customer-facing indexes (C5-1b) are §0.7 items owned by the cross-currency programme (§8, decision D2 there), not by Business Search; they are listed so the fixing session does not build them without the table.

---

#### Details

##### AG1-01 — HIGH — the catalogue tool truncates a complete match set to 5 rows with no count

`ProviderCatalogSearchService.cs:834-841`:
```csharp
return new CatalogLookupResult {
    Outcome = VoiceCatalogOutcome.Matches,
    TotalMatches = total,
    Offerings = items.Take(_settings.LookupMaxResults).ToList(),
```
`Clinqet.API/appsettings.json` `Voice:Catalog` → `"LookupMaxResults": 5` (voice ergonomics: "read out at most three at a time"). The index page is read at `LookupTooBroadThreshold + 1` = 26 rows (`:369`), then cut to 5.

`SearchServicesTool.cs:230-261` re-wraps the payload only in the `tooBroad` branch (adds `totalMatches`/`showing`) and the `notSearchedIn` branch; the plain `Matches` branch ships `{"services":[…]}` — `merged.TotalMatches` is never written, no sample note.

**Reproduction.** 12 approved services whose name contains "excavator"; member asks "list our excavators". Index leg: `page.Total = 12 ≤ 25` → `Shape` → `Matches`, `TotalMatches = 12`, `Offerings = 5`. Cosmos `UnapprovedOnly` leg → 0. `MergeCatalogLegs` → 5 offerings, `Matches`. Payload: five rows, nothing else. System-prompt rule 4 tells the model to quote "exactly as the tool result gives them" → *"You have 5 excavators: …"* stated with confidence. Same on a non-indexed stamp (Cosmos leg, `NeedsExactTotal = true`, `TotalCount = 12`, `Offerings = 5`, `TotalIsExact = true` — the exact total is in hand and discarded).

**Class.** Silent truncation — the failure the SKILL names in `BusinessSearchToolBase.cs:129-131` ("a silent truncation is what made the model claim a partial list was complete"). Group B closed it with `Bound(rows, trueTotal)` (`BusinessSearchWorkToolBase.cs:422-437`); Group A's list tool never got the same shape. No test covers >5 matches (`grep LookupMaxResults Clinqet.API.UnitTests` = 0 hits; `BusinessSearchCatalogRetrievalTests` has no `totalMatches` assertion outside TooBroad).

**Source of truth.** `CatalogLookupResult.TotalMatches` + `TotalIsExact`.

**Root fix.** The tool asks for a screen-sized page (`CatalogLookupQuery.MaxResults`, a `BusinessSearch` dial; the voice caller keeps 5), and whenever `Offerings.Count < TotalMatches` — or `!TotalIsExact` with a filled page — writes `total`/`shown` + the `Bound` sample note. One truthfulness shape for every list tool. Not a schema change.

##### AG1-02 — HIGH — a failed leg is dropped from the catalogue union

`SearchServicesTool.cs:291-294`:
```csharp
if (legs.All(l => l.Outcome == VoiceCatalogOutcome.Unavailable))
    return legs[0] with { Outcome = VoiceCatalogOutcome.Unavailable, ... };
var usable = legs.Where(l => l.Outcome != VoiceCatalogOutcome.Unavailable).ToList();
```
`notSearchedIn` (`:152-156`) is built ONLY from `renderings.RejectedLanguages` and `!scripts.AlphabetsResolved` — never from a lost leg. The service never throws for this: `ProviderCatalogSearchService.cs:193-214` return `Unavailable()` on the whole-ladder timeout, `CatalogIsolationException` and any other exception, so `Task.WhenAll` (`:144`) completes and the base's C6 catch (`BusinessSearchToolBase.cs:109-114`) never fires. Contrast `SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:250-260`, which adds `"{n} of {m} searches did not complete"` from `LegsRequested/LegsSucceeded`.

**Reproduction A** (indexed stamp): Cosmos throttled → the `Cosmos/UnapprovedOnly` leg returns `Unavailable`; the `Index` leg answers the approved rows → `Matches`, no note → a pending draft the member asked about is reported as not in the catalogue. **B:** the `Index` call's 8 s ladder (`LookupTimeoutMs`) expires → `Unavailable`; the `UnapprovedOnly` leg answers 0 rows → `merged.Outcome = None` → `:163-165` writes `note = NothingFound` ("Nothing in the business's own information covers that… produce no number or price") about an approved service that exists. **C** (no index, two renderings): one Cosmos leg fails → the other rendering's rows are "the whole search".

**Class.** Partial fan-out (§11.3): "a leg that failed … mean[s] the search covered less than it set out to". Here the two store-legs are disjoint POPULATIONS (approved vs unapproved), so a lost leg is half the catalogue, not a lost synonym. **Source of truth:** each `CatalogLookupResult.Outcome`. **Root fix:** the merge carries per-leg failure (count + store) into the result; a lost store-leg goes through `PartialSearch`/`PartialSearchFoundNothing` + `notSearchedIn` (e.g. "the published offerings could not be searched"), and when every leg of the asked rendering is lost the tool returns `TemporarilyUnavailable` (C6).

##### AG1-03 — Medium — a failed answer still spends the daily allowance

`BusinessSearchController.cs:112-113` runs `EnforceLimitsAsync` (increments at `:424` and `:431`) BEFORE `_agent.StreamAsync` (`:140`). Every terminal `Error` in the agent happens after that spend: the token window (`BusinessSearchAgent.cs:159-182`, checked on iteration 1 before any model call), C6 `Unavailable` (`:298-308`), `NoAnswer` (`:253-263`), `TookTooLong` (`:389-395`), generic (`:344-361`). `BusinessSearchUsageCounterRepository.cs` has `Increment` only; `IBusinessSearchUsageCounter` has no decrement. The controller's own comment (`:375-376`) — "a refused question never spends the member's allowance" — holds only for the controller's refusals.

**Reproduction.** `AIAssistant:RateLimiting:BusinessSearch.TokensPerWindow = 100000` per BUSINESS per minute (`appsettings.json:2221-2225`; `EnableTokenBasedLimiting: true` at `:2214`). A follow-up conversation costs 40–60k prompt tokens per answer (12 replayed turns × ≤4 000 chars + 17 schemas + ≤18 000 chars of tool results, 2–3 model turns). The third colleague to ask within a minute receives `BusinessSearch_Error_TooFast` — *"You're asking a little too quickly"* — for their FIRST question of the day, and `IncrementMemberAsync` has already counted it against their 60 and the business's 200. Same for a Cosmos blip.

**Class.** Every terminal `Error` frame; plus the copy: the bucket is the business's, the sentence blames the member. **Source of truth:** the two `AiUsageCounter` docs. **Root fix:** reverse the spend on `Error && !wroteAnswer` (`PatchOperation.Increment("/count", -1)` on the existing docs — no schema change), keep the pre-check; a per-business token refusal gets its own sentence.

##### AG1-04 — Medium — Group B citations survive the payload cap

`BusinessSearchWorkToolBase.cs:452-469` — `CapPayload` returns only the JSON, drops rows from the tail, writes `rowsShortenedForLength`, never touches `meta["shown"]`. Every Group B tool then returns `Citations = citationDtos` unfiltered: `ListBookingsTool.cs:160`, `ListQuotesTool.cs:156`, `ListInvoicesTool.cs:152`, `ListLeadsTool.cs:144`, `ListCustomersTool.cs:172`, `InboxSummaryTool.cs:162`, `ListTeamMembersTool.cs:124`, `ListReviewsTool.cs:158`, `SearchCallFollowUpsTool.cs:153`, `SearchRefundRequestsTool.cs:184`. Group A does `citationDtos.Take(kept)` (`SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:191`, `SearchServicesTool.cs:278`, `ListOffersTool.cs:99`).

**Reachable:** `MaxRows 10`, `RowTextMaxChars 400`, `ToolResultMaxChars 6000`. A review row (`title` ≤400 + `comment` ≤400 + who/dates) ≈ 850 chars → 10 rows ≈ 8.5 k; a booking with four 400-char service names ≈ 1.7 k/row; the refund tool's own comment (`:152-156`) says its rows exceed the cap.

**Reproduction.** 10 reviews with 300-char comments → payload keeps ~6 rows, `rowsShortenedForLength: 4`, `reviews.shown: 10` (contradiction inside one payload), and 10 `Citation` frames reach the client, which draws every one (`clinqetwebpartnerapp/src/hooks/useBusinessSearch.js:176-178`) → four source cards no `[n]` refers to. **Class:** SKILL §8 "citations are emitted only for the rows that … reached the model" — false for ten of seventeen tools; `BusinessSearchToolBase.cs:124-126` names this exact failure. No test pins it (`rowsShortenedForLength` appears in no unit test). **Root fix:** `CapPayload` returns `(json, kept)` like `SerializeCapped`, corrects `shown`, and the base exposes one `Finish(payload, rowsKey, citations)` so ten tools cannot diverge.

##### AG1-05 — Medium — an e-mail address as a colleague's name

`MemberLifecycleService.cs:986-990`: `BuildDisplayName` → `email ?? string.Empty` when first/last are blank (the normal state of a freshly accepted invite). `ListTeamMembersTool.cs:84` `var name = Text(member.DisplayName)`; `:90` `Title = name.Length == 0 ? member.MembershipId : name`; `:106` `name` in the model payload. The tool's own header (`:17-18`) says "NO e-mail and NO phone number". `BusinessSearchRosterService.cs:104-114` already holds the guard (`SafeName`: an `@` ⇒ the membership id) and its comment names this tool as the projection it protects.

**Reproduction.** Invite `gaurav@example.com`; they accept without a name; ask "who is on my team?" → payload `name: "gaurav@example.com"`, card title `gaurav@example.com`, and the answer sentence carries it. The existing guard test (`Clinqet.API.UnitTests/Services/BusinessSearch/BusinessSearchGroupBToolTests.cs:523`) sets `DisplayName = "Gaurav Shah"` explicitly, so the fallback path is untested. **Class:** rule-19 PII projection; two definitions of the display-name rule. **Root fix:** one shared name projection (move `SafeName` to core; use it in the tool, the agent's PEOPLE block and the redactor) + a fixture with no first/last name.

##### AG1-06 — Medium — the model is the money renderer for Group B

`ListBookingsTool.cs:144-145` `total = booking.Price?.TotalAmount, currency = booking.Price?.Currency`; same shape at `ListQuotesTool.cs:134-135`, `ListInvoicesTool.cs:134-135`, `InvoiceTotalsTool.cs:118-136` (`total = paidSplit.Amount`, `s.TotalAmount`, `summary.GrandTotal`, `o.Amount`), `ListOffersTool.cs:86,91`. Versus `SearchServicesTool.cs:211` (`BusinessSearchMoney.Format` → `CurrencyMinorUnit.FormatMajor`, reader culture) and `SearchRefundRequestsTool.cs:195-199` (`CurrencyMinorUnit.Format`, reader culture; its comment: "ALREADY FORMATTED, deliberately. Handing over minor units plus a currency invites the model to…").

**Reproduction.** Hindi reader: "what did booking BK-1042 cost and what does a facial cost?" → the service arrives as `₹5,31,000.00`; the booking as `531000` + `INR` and the model writes `531000 INR` or `₹531,000.00` — a code instead of a symbol, or English grouping, in the same paragraph as a platform-rendered figure. A CAD booking at an Indian business: the model may write `$1,000` (the `$`-over-C$ defect of §18.9, re-created by the model). **Class:** §17.2/§18.6 "ONE definition … the symbol follows the currency, the grouping follows the reader"; rule 4 forbids the model recalculating yet the tools make it render. **Root fix:** pre-render every Group B amount with `CurrencyMinorUnit.FormatMajor(amount, code, LanguageCulture.Resolve(language))` — number alone via `TryParse` when the code is unknown (§18.10) — keeping the ISO code beside it for the model.

##### AG1-07 — Low — the asked script counted as "not searched"

`BusinessSearchQueryRenderings.cs:127-133`: for the asked script with a wrong-script rendering, `accepted.Add(question)` AND `rejected.Add(script)`; `RejectedLanguages` feeds `notSearchedIn` (`SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:253`, `SearchServicesTool.cs:153`). Reproduction: Gujarati question, model fills `queryInGujarati` with Latin → the Gujarati leg runs with the question, yet the payload says `notSearchedIn: ["Gujarati"]` + `PartialSearch` → the answer tells the member part of their Gujarati material could not be searched. False, in the safe direction. Root fix: a `Substituted` list distinct from `Rejected`.

##### AG1-08 — Low — failed roster read ⇒ "could not tell who they meant"

`BusinessSearchRosterService.cs:121` iterates `await ListAsync(...)`, which returns `[]` on failure (`:86-94`); `BusinessSearchWorkToolBase.cs:203-210` turns null into `MemberNotFound` → `MemberNotResolved`. The interface (`IBusinessSearchRosterService.cs:17-19`) says an empty list "is AMBIGUOUS and callers must not treat it as…" — `ResolveAsync` is such a caller. Reproduction: PEOPLE list from the 60 s cache; cache expires; SQL blips during the tool → a real colleague is "not matched" instead of C6. Root fix: `ResolveAsync` over `TryListAsync`, tri-state, `ReadFailed` ⇒ `TemporarilyUnavailable`.

##### AG1-09 — Low — validation language

`BusinessSearchController.cs:102` `return ValidationFailed().Result!` runs before `:106` `var language = ResolveLanguage(request.Language)`; `BaseController.cs:777-786` `ValidationFailed()` uses `GetPreferredLanguage()` (Accept-Language). `ReportNotHelpful` same (`:301` vs `:303`). Web `businessSearchService.js:20-28` sends no `Accept-Language`. Reproduction: Gujarati UI on web, browser `en-US`, a 501-char question → `Error_BusinessSearchQuestionTooLong` in English — the bug the controller's comment (`:103-105`) says it fixed. Root fix: resolve first (`ResolveLanguage(request?.Language)`), pass into a `ValidationFailed(language)` overload.

##### AG1-10 — Low — role keys reach the model

`ListTeamMembersTool.cs:108-110` `roles = member.Roles?.Select(r => r.RoleKey)` while the card (`:92`) uses `BusinessRole_{key}_Name`. Asked "what role does Gaurav have?", the model prints `operations_manager` (§16.2 rule 4: NEVER PRINT A ROLE KEY; the model has no map to consult). Root fix: send the localized name.

##### AG1-11 — Low — two business-currency resolvers

`FullProviderContextService.cs:192-194` `var currency = profile.ResolvePrimaryCurrency(_discoverySettings)` → `DiscoverySettings.CountryCurrencyMap` (`DiscoverySettings.cs:42-55`) → `GetBusinessProfileTool.cs:76`, `ListOffersTool.cs:91`. `InvoiceService.cs:359` `_currencyService.GetCurrencyCodeAsync` → `CountryResolutionService` ISO-4217 map → `InvoiceTotalsTool.cs:113`. Any country the two maps disagree on (the SKILL §18.8 records the fixture case "summary USD, payload CAD") yields one answer naming two currencies for one business. Latent on CA/US/IN today. Root fix: `FullProviderContextService` resolves through `ICurrencyService.GetCurrencyCodeAsync`.

##### AG1-12 — Low — image dedupe per call

`SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:110` `seenImages` is per call; `BusinessSearchAgent.cs:444-453` caps by count only. Two calls citing the same picture → two SAS mints (`:330-331`) and two `Image` frames; the client dedupes (`useBusinessSearch.js:184`, mobile `:202`) but the second frame already consumed a `MaxImagesPerAnswer` slot (`:446-447`), so an answer can show fewer than four distinct pictures. Root fix: an answer-level set keyed `docId:imageId`, like `emitted` for citations, checked before the mint.

##### AG1-13 — Low — a per-leg dial that never binds

`ProviderKnowledgeSearchService.Provider.cs:62-64` `deadline.CancelAfter(RetrievalTimeoutMs)` = `Voice:Knowledge:RetrievalTimeoutMs = 4000`; the per-leg clock (`:186-188`, `Retrieval:PerLegTimeoutSeconds = 20`) is linked under it. The SKILL §14.2/settings comment ("each leg's own clock … a slow leg cannot hold the answer") describes a dial that cannot take effect; a leg slower than 4 s is cut by the phone budget, and when the embedding/visibility read is the slow part the whole answer is `Unavailable` (`:154-161`). Root fix: the provider path takes its fan-out budget from `BusinessSearch:Retrieval`, bounded by `TotalTimeoutSeconds`, or the dial is deleted (§4 orphan rule).

##### AG1-14 — Low — narrowing never echoed; unknown group dropped silently

`ProviderCatalogSearchService.cs:769-773` logs "searching without it" and returns an empty `ResolvedGroup`; no flag on the result. `SearchServicesTool.cs:186-228` echoes none of `group`/`minPrice`/`maxPrice`. "excavators under 50000 in Landscaping" with a group the catalogue lacks → the whole price-filtered catalogue, presented as Landscaping. Contrast `BusinessSearchWorkToolBase.cs:365-381` (`StatusOf`/`Filtered`: "a usable one is echoed into the payload so 'filtered' is never invisible"). Root fix: `GroupApplied`/`GroupIgnored` on the result and a `filter` line in the payload.

##### AG1-15 — Low — "no profile" told as "temporarily unavailable"

`FullProviderContextService.cs:160-165` returns null only for a missing profile (every other failure throws); `ListOffersTool.cs:50-51`, `GetBusinessProfileTool.cs:54-55`, `GetAvailabilityTool.cs:45-46` map that null to `TemporarilyUnavailable`. Rare (profile auto-created on first dashboard access); the honest note is the `FeatureOff` class.

##### AG1-16 — Low — `TimeZoneAssumed` misses the invalid-id case

`BusinessSearchDateRangeResolver.cs:351-352` `assumed = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(profile?.TimeZoneId)`; `BookingTimeHelper.ResolveTimeZoneId` (`clinqetcore/Utilities/BookingTimeHelper.cs:18-25`) falls through to the address when the stored id does not resolve. Stored `"Asia/Nowhere"` → days derived from the address, payload note omitted. Root fix: `assumed = !string.Equals(resolvedId, profile.TimeZoneId)`.

##### AG1-17 — Low — stale contract (SKILL / comments)

- SKILL §8 "Citations are emitted only for the rows that actually survived the payload cap" — false for Group B (AG1-04).
- SKILL §14.2 "`LocalStartWall`/`LocalEndWallInclusive` for wall-clock fields" — no tool uses them; tools pass `LocalFrom/LocalTo` and the repositories derive bounds (`BookingRepository.cs:279-280`, `QuoteRepository.cs:173-174`, `InvoiceRepository.cs:152-153` via `DateRangeBounds.FromWallClockDays`). The three members at `clinqetcore/Interfaces/BusinessSearch/BusinessSearchDateRange.cs:22-28` are read only by `BusinessSearchDateRangeResolverTests.cs:130-132` — dead code (§22.2).
- SKILL §9 "burst 6/min via AiRateLimitingService (`AIAssistant:RateLimiting:BusinessSearch`…)" — the burst is `BusinessSearch:Limits:QuestionsPerMinute` supplied by the controller (`:382-389`); the `AIAssistant` section feeds only the token window (`AiRateLimitingService.cs:89-91, 224-229`).
- SKILL §6 "deduplicated per answer" — per call (AG1-12).
- SKILL §18.1 "A second answer is now structurally impossible" — AG1-11.
- `ServiceRepository.cs:305-310` "`approvalStatus` is NOT an included path … NOT assumed" — stale: `clinqetcore/Cosmos/Setup/CosmosContainerPolicies.cs:42` includes `/approvalStatus/?` (SKILL §19.6 is right; the comment is not).
- `CurrencyTotals.cs:114-117` and `CurrencyService.cs:64-65` inline `Enum.TryParse + IsDefined` instead of `CurrencyMinorUnit.TryParse` — correct today (IsDefined present) but three copies of the "ONE parse" §18.6 warns about.

- **Agent loop** (`BusinessSearchAgent.cs`): fragments accumulate unconditionally, completeness checked at harvest (`:245-247, 641-652`); same-round prose carried as `Content` (`:282-290`); `finish_reason: length` ⇒ `Status{partial:true}` then `Done` (`:268-278`), and mid-tool-call truncation ⇒ `BadArguments` via `BusinessSearchToolBase.cs:96-99`; token window on every iteration, half-written ⇒ partial+Done, nothing written ⇒ `Error AI_RATE_LIMITED` (`:159-182`); last iteration and char-budget exhaustion offer no tools (`:196-199`); model-leg timeout has its own catch and terminal frame (`:328-338`); budget expiry keeps text (`:318-327`); client abort writes nothing (`:339-343`); tool throw ⇒ `TemporarilyUnavailable` ⇒ C6 `Error` (`ToolBase:109-114`, agent `:298-308`); `MaxToolCallsPerIteration` `Take` keeps assistant/tool message pairing consistent (`:245-290`); duplicate calls dedupe citations through the registry + `emitted` (`:432-440`, registry `:23-36`); bounded channel `Wait` + linked CTS, producer awaited with `SuppressThrowing` (`:72-91`); `writer.Complete()` in `finally`; Meta first with one `Guid.N` per `ProduceAsync` (`:143-152`); traced every path — exactly one terminal frame, `Error` never followed by `Done`.
- **Model client**: `reasoning_effort: "none"` constant (`:20,61`), `stream_options.include_usage` (`:218-222`), no temperature, `ReadLineAsync` (not `EndOfStream`), `[DONE]`/comment/blank skipped (`:113-116`), per-leg `CancelAfter` linked to the answer deadline (`:87-88`), `AcquireAsync` is a no-op for `Interactive` lane (`AiBudgetGovernor.cs:65`).
- **SSE wire** (item 3): `SseJsonOptions` camelCase + `WhenWritingNull`, enums PascalCase via `[JsonConverter(JsonStringEnumConverter)]` on `BusinessSearchEventType`, `BusinessSearchCitationKind`, `ErrorCode` (`ErrorCode.cs:5`); `hasFullText` omitted when false (`WhenWritingDefault`) and both clients treat absence as false; `: ping\n\n` every 15 s (`:40-41, 153-160`) skipped by both parsers (`askRules.js:79-89`, `askRules.ts` identical, `sseStream.ts:42-53`); web/mobile `citationAction` are byte-identical bar quote style (diffed); clients read `text, citation, image, partial, answerId, sessionId` (`useBusinessSearch.js:157-194`, `.ts:176-212`); `citation.reason/excerpt/hasFullText/page/recordId`, image `docId/imageId/thumbUrl/url/caption` typed in `askRules.ts:78-83`. `TokensUsed` is `[JsonIgnore]` and read off the frame by the controller only.
- **Query renderings** (item 4): both callers pass the right flag — `SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:78` default `true` (no other narrowing), `SearchServicesTool.cs:85-86` `!narrowedOtherwise`; canonical field order by `TextScriptDetector.OrderOf` (`:35-37`); the all-empty filter-only case is not partial (`:139`); catalogue rule appended after the measured clause (`:58`).
- **Dates** (item 5): exactly-one-of (`:231-234`); bookings/quotes/invoices pass `LocalFrom/LocalTo` (DateOnly) and the repositories build half-open wall-clock day bounds (`DateRangeBounds.FromWallClockDays` `:27-29` = `[from 00:00, next midnight)`); `paidAt` `>= @startDate AND < @endDate` with `StartUtc/EndUtcExclusive` (`InvoiceRepository.cs:351`, tool `:76-77`); leads/calls `createdAt >= @from AND < @to` (`BroadcastProviderRepository.cs:93`, `CallSummaryRepository.cs:115`); refunds SQL `CreatedAt >= from && < to` (`BookingDisputeService.cs:446`); DST nudge bounded 12×15 min (`:283-290`); tz cache `Size = 1`, failed profile read NOT cached (`:339-358`); `9999-12-31` refused (`:265-266`).
- **Roster** (item 6): `SafeName` in the roster (`:109-114`); `MemberNotFound` explicit (`WorkToolBase:159-163, 204-210`); `TryListAsync` keeps failure distinct and uncached (`:86-94`); `MaxNamesInPrompt` ⇒ pointer to `list_team_members` (agent `:621-627`); `IsSolo` resolved only when a tool can narrow by member, before schemas (`:121-122`), fail-toward-team and uncached (`:48-55`); no schema carries a member NAME (only `assignedToMemberId`, `WorkToolBase:106-113`).
- **Money helpers** (item 7): `InvoiceTotalsTool` count/`countInCurrency`/`otherCurrencies` match `InvoiceSummaryDto` (Count = every invoice, `PrimaryCurrencyCount`, `OtherCurrencies`) and the summary currency is `ICurrencyService` (`InvoiceService.cs:359, 406-407`, never null); `CurrencyTotals.Split` lists a group with rows even at zero amount (`:143-144`); `CurrencyMinorUnit.TryParse` has `Enum.IsDefined` (`:58-59`); `BusinessSearchMoney` uses `TryParse` and renders the number alone when unknown (`:362-365`); the `PriceText` fallback at `SearchServicesTool.cs:213` is unreachable (both formatters null in the same cases — `FormatPriceText` `:866-885` vs `Format` `:324-329`); refund amounts `CurrencyMinorUnit.Format(minor, CurrencyCode, reader culture)` with `dispute.Currency` typed `CurrencyCode` (`BookingPaymentDispute.cs:37`).

No `enum.ToString()` reaches a card.
- **recordId vs routing** (item 9): booking → number (`:118,128`; web `/myBookings/{recordId}`, mobile `bookingDetails.tsx:163` fetches by `bookingNumber || bookingId`); quote → id (web `/myQuotes/{id}` = `notificationNavigation.js:87`, mobile `quoteInfo.quoteId`); invoice → `invoiceNumber ?? invoiceId` (web `/invoices/{recordId}`, mobile `invoiceNumber`); lead → `broadcastId` (both); conversation → `conversationId` (both); review → `reviewId` (web query; mobile list); `Invoice` kind with no recordId (`InvoiceTotalsTool`) → `"invoices"` list on both. Customer/Call/Refund/Member cards open LIST pages on both apps (`BusinessSearchPage.jsx:255,273,284,289`; mobile `:238,260,270,275`) — the server's `RecordId` for those four kinds is used only to decide that an action exists; whether M5 approved list-opening is the UI auditor's call.
- **Controller** (item 10): `[RequiresPermission("business.profile.read", Business)]` on all five actions; 500-char cap via `[MaxLength]`; `IsMintedId` = `Guid.TryParseExact("N")` for session and answer ids (`:481-483`); feedback catch is exact — `CosmosDbRepository.AddItemAsync` wraps ONLY 409 as `InvalidOperationException` (`Base/CosmosDbRepository.cs:225-231`), everything else rethrows to the middleware; deterministic `bsfb_{answerId}_{membershipId}`; own rate bucket; `sessions/{id}` member-scoped through `Metadata["membershipId"]` (`McpSessionService.cs:111-114`), foreign id ⇒ new conversation; `pages/{page}` and `text` authorize via the point-read + `KnowledgeSearchAudienceRule` (`DocumentService:124-149`); `prepare` 204; `ApiResponse.Fail/NotFound/Ok` envelope; every message a key. Partial marker appended only to a stored cut-short turn; `Trim`/`FollowUpTurns*2` agree with the agent's window (`:134-139` vs agent `:521`).
- **Settings** (item 11): scripted comparison of `BusinessSearch` in `appsettings.json` against `BusinessSearchSettings` class defaults — every dial equal, `SystemPrompt` byte-identical (1331 chars), no extra keys, no orphan (`ServiceLookupSource` gone). `AiModels.Luna = "gpt-5.6-luna"`. Guarded by `BusinessSearchConventionTests.TheAppsettingsBlock_MirrorsTheClassDefaults`.
- **Reused seams**: `SearchForProviderAsync` — `ReadFailed` ⇒ `Unavailable`, empty allow-list ⇒ `None` without a search, all legs failed ⇒ `Unavailable`, allow-list in the FILTER, post-filter belt (`:93-138`); `HasTextAsync` false on store failure (`KnowledgeContentArtifactStore.cs:79-92`); `Published` read off the row (`ProviderCatalogSearchService.cs:558-560`), index rows default `true`; `SelectFields` includes `currency` (`:56`); `NeedsExactTotal`/`TotalIsExact` (`ServiceRepository.cs:271-279`) and the tool prints a figure only when `legs.Length == 1 && TotalIsExact` (`:240-244`); `MergeCatalogLegs` dedupes by `ServiceId` (`:296-299`); `IndexAvailable` gates the second leg (`:109-141`); feature gates equal the pages' (`ProviderDisputesController.cs:113`, `AnalyticsController.cs:70-72`, `BroadcastProviderController.cs:68`); Insights tier mapping identical to `AnalyticsController.cs:81-84`; reviews: the tool's direct repo call equals the page's service path for the business's own members (`ReviewService.cs:383-399` with `canSeeUnmoderatedReviews = true` per `ReviewController.cs:69-79`); customers narrowing mirrors `CustomerController.cs:218-232`; narrowing reaches the COUNT (`WorkListNarrowing.cs:6-9`, repositories branch on `MatchesNothing`).

### 18.2 Authorization and tenant isolation (AG2)

### AG2 — Authorization & tenant isolation, Business Search (read-only audit, 2026-09-05)

**Contract note:** `AUTHORIZATION-DESIGN.md` §6b holds **C1–C6** (L228–294). The owner's brief says "C1–C7"; the document wins — there is no C7. Nothing was built or run; every claim below is from reading the current files. No scratch files were written.

#### 1. Findings

| ID | Severity | file:line | One line |
|---|---|---|---|
| AG2-01 | **Medium — DEFECT** | `clinqetinfrastructure/Services/BusinessSearch/Tools/ListReviewsTool.cs:66-79` | `list_reviews` returns **Pending and Rejected** reviews; the provider's Reviews page shows **Approved only**. Golden rule "never more" (T3 class). |
| AG2-02 | Low — DEFECT (unreachable today) | `clinqetinfrastructure/Services/BusinessSearch/BusinessSearchWorkToolBase.cs:213-225` | `mineOnly` overwrites a `MatchesNothing` narrowing with `AssignedToMembership(mine)`; the evaluator still drops every row, but a query the page never issues runs and the empty note says "assigned to you". |
| AG2-03 | Low — contract deviation (conservative) | `clinqetinfrastructure/Services/BusinessSearch/Tools/ListCustomersTool.cs:123` | Contact detail gated on `customer.read` **at Business**; a contractor/technician (Assigned) gets no email/phone through Search although the Customers page shows them for assigned customers (§7.19 "covering scope"; "never less"). Needs owner ruling. |
| AG2-04 | Low — abuse/cost | `clinqetapi/Clinqet.API/Controllers/BusinessSearch/BusinessSearchController.cs:307-361` | `feedback/not-helpful` has a 6/min burst and **no daily cap**; `answerId` is any client-chosen Guid-N; each call writes a durable AdminAlert row (≤8.5 KB, 90 d) ⇒ ~8,640 arbitrary-text admin rows per member per day. |
| AG2-05 | Low — cost | `BusinessSearchController.cs:248-257`, `BusinessAlphabetService.cs:112-117` | `prepare` has no rate limit and an *Unresolved* alphabet lookup is never cached ⇒ a business whose facet fails re-issues two index queries per call (single-flighted, but unbounded sequentially). |
| AG2-06 | Medium — test gap | §7 item 2 | The page-vs-tool "golden rule" comparison exists **only** for refund requests (`TheToolReturnsExactlyTheRowsTheRefundRequestsPageReturns`). Nothing compares bookings/quotes/invoices/customers/inbox/reviews/calls/leads to their page — which is exactly how AG2-01 shipped. |
| AG2-07 | Low — test gaps | §7 items 7, 9, 10, 11 | No test for an escaped/foreign image blob path at `SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:315`; no test that a document restricted between turn 1 and turn 2 vanishes; refusal-copy pinned only as "no ids in the note"; "no number" pinned at tool level only; no API-suite test injects a foreign row into the **catalogue** index response (only the knowledge index). |

No finding needs a §0.7 schema change. **No cross-partition Cosmos operation exists on the path** (§4).

#### 2. Defect details

##### AG2-01 — `list_reviews` serves moderation-hidden reviews (Medium)
**Reproduction.** Any member holding `review.read` (owner, administrator, operations_manager, sales_representative, catalog_manager, read_only_auditor — `TenancyRoleCatalogDefinition.cs:199-303`) asks "what are customers saying about us?". The tool builds
```
ListReviewsTool.cs:66-76  Filter = new Shared.Models.Filters.ReviewFilter { BusinessId = …, MinRating = …, MaxRating = …, HasReply = awaitingReply == true ? false : null
                          // ‼️ NO status filter, because the provider's own Reviews screen has none: it reads the
                          // public routes with the unmoderated flag set, so it shows pending and rejected rows too.
ListReviewsTool.cs:79     var page = await _reviews.GetPaginatedReviewsAsync(authorization.BusinessId, query, null, cancellationToken);
```
`ReviewFilter.Status` is `string?` with no default (`BusinessFilters.cs:155-206`), so `ReviewRepository.cs:747-756` adds **no status predicate**: `Pending` and `Rejected` rows (`ReviewStatus.cs:6-11`) come back and are quoted verbatim (`ListReviewsTool.cs:138-139 title = Text(review.Title), comment = Text(review.Comment)`).

**The page.** Partner web `Reviews.jsx:107` → `profileServices.js:100-105 getApi.get(\`${url.PublicReviewsAPI(businessId)}…\`)` → `ReviewController.cs:129-130 _reviewService.GetPaginatedReviewsAsync(businessId, queryDto, currentUserId, await CanSeeUnmoderatedReviewsAsync(businessId))`, where
```
ReviewController.cs:69-79  var userNumber = User.FindFirstValue("UserNumber");
                           if (… string.Equals(userNumber, businessId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
                           return await GetCurrentUserTypeAsync() == UserType.Admin;
```
`clinqet-provider-teams/SKILL.md:1876-1879`: *"A BusinessId CAN NEVER EQUAL A UserNumber … six characters versus five … So `u.UserNumber == businessId` is never true"* — so for every provider member the flag is **false**, and `ReviewService.cs:390-395` sets `IncludeOwnUserId = currentUserId` ⇒ `ReviewRepository.cs:749 "(c.status = @approvedStatus OR c.userId = @ownUserId)"`. The page shows Approved (plus the member's own authored review); the tool's premise in its comment is false.

**Class:** T3 (rows the page cannot show). **Source of truth:** the status rule in `ReviewService.GetPaginatedReviewsAsync` (L376-395). **Root fix:** the tool must run the page's own rule — call `IReviewService.GetPaginatedReviewsAsync(businessId, query, currentUserId: null, canSeeUnmoderatedReviews: false)` (which sets `Status = Approved`) instead of the repository — or, if the owner rules that members should see pending/rejected, make `CanSeeUnmoderatedReviewsAsync` tenancy-aware (`Tenant?.Has("review.read")`) so the page widens and the tool follows; never a second copy of the predicate in the tool. Add the page-vs-tool row comparison (AG2-06) seeded with one Pending and one Rejected review. No schema change.

##### AG2-02 — the possessive widens a `MatchesNothing` scope (Low, unreachable today)
`WorkListNarrowing.cs:53-55  PermissionScope.Team => tenant.TeamIds is { Count: > 0 } ? … : MatchesNothing`. In `ResolveScopeAsync`:
```
WorkToolBase.cs:213-225  string? mine = null; if (member is null && possessive && !isSolo) mine = authorization.MembershipId;
                         var effective = narrowing; … else if (mine is not null) effective = new WorkListNarrowing(WorkListNarrowingKind.AssignedToMembership, mine);
```
The tools test `scope.Narrowing.Kind == MatchesNothing` on the *effective* value (`ListBookingsTool.cs:57`), so the guard is bypassed and `BookingRepository.cs:204-205` (which returns without a query for `MatchesNothing`) is not reached. No leak: `KeepInScope` runs because `NarrowsTheQuery` is true and `ResourceScopeEvaluator.cs:50 if (context.TeamIds.Count == 0) return ScopeDecision.DeniedOutOfScope;` drops every row; total becomes 0 and `EmptyNoteFor` says "nothing assigned to you" (`MineMembershipId is not null`) where the page says nothing at all. Team scope is granted by no role today. **Root fix:** return the `MatchesNothing` scope before applying `member`/`mine` (`if (narrowing.Kind == WorkListNarrowingKind.MatchesNothing) return new WorkScope { Narrowing = narrowing, IsSolo = isSolo };`) and pin it in `BusinessSearchWorkToolTests`.

##### AG2-03 — contact detail withheld from Assigned-scope members (Low, documented deviation)
`ListCustomersTool.cs:120-123 // ‼️ Contact detail rides ONLY on a covering customer.read … var withContact = authorization.Has("customer.read", PermissionScope.Business);`. Contractor and technician hold `customer.read` at **Assigned** (`TenancyRoleCatalogDefinition.cs:250-253, 289-295`, comment: *"Full customer detail but Assigned scope ONLY (G2): a technician who cannot phone the customer … cannot do the job"*), and the tool already intersects rows to their assigned customers (L81-92), so their Customers page shows email/phone for exactly those rows. §1 "never less, either" and §7.19 "unless the caller holds `customer.read` at a covering scope" both point the other way. The code comment states a deliberate product choice ("a search answer is broadcast into a conversation"). Owner must rule; if the page is the rule, `withContact = authorization.Has("customer.read")` at any scope is the one-line fix.

##### AG2-04 — not-helpful endpoint: unbounded durable writes with client-chosen ids (Low)
`BusinessSearchController.cs:307-310 if (!IsMintedId(request.AnswerId)) return BadRequest(…)` accepts **any** Guid-N; L314-321 is a 1-minute burst only; L334-361 writes `AlertId = $"bsfb_{answerId}_{tenant.MembershipId}"`, `Description = $"Question: {request.Question.Trim()}\n\nAnswer: {request.Answer.Trim()}"` (DTO caps 500/8000 chars, `BusinessSearchDtos.cs:30,34`), `Ttl = … FeedbackAlertTtlDays * 86400` (90 d). Neither daily counter is read. The `answerId` minted on the Meta frame (`BusinessSearchAgent.cs:143-152`) is stored nowhere, so it cannot be validated without a new field (that option would be §0.7). **Root fix without schema:** apply the member daily counter (`_usage.GetMemberCountAsync`) or a dedicated daily feedback cap before the write.

##### AG2-05 — `prepare` unlimited; negative alphabet lookups uncached (Low)
`BusinessSearchController.cs:248-257` has no limiter; `BusinessAlphabetService.cs:112-117 if (result.Resolved) { _cache.Set(…) }` — an `Unresolved` set (either facet failed, L141) is never cached, so every subsequent `prepare`/`ask` re-runs `FacetAsync` ×2 (L135-136). Single-flight (L97-100) bounds concurrency only. **Fix:** same per-member burst bucket as `ask`; cache `Unresolved` with a short TTL.

#### 5. §7 coverage (the 20 required tests)

Present: 1 (matrix, 98), 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16 (owner "my" case), 17, 18, 19, 20. Partial/missing: **2** (only refunds — AG2-06), **7** (foreign docId/page ✓; foreign imageRef ✗; foreign row → discard+alarm ✓ knowledge only, ✗ catalogue), **9** (✗ no restricted-between-turns test), **10** (ids-only assertion; no scan of the refusal keys), **11** (tool-level only; the answer-level rule lives in the prompt and cannot be pinned with a scripted model).

#### 6. Not verified

- Nothing was built or executed; test results and DI resolution at runtime are inferred from source only.
- Bodies of `ConversationRepository` (inbox reads), `BroadcastProviderRepository`/`CallSummaryRepository` constructors (container names), `FullProviderContextService`, `ProviderInsightsRepository` beyond `GetRollupAsync`, `BusinessProfileRepository`, `CategoryRepository` — partition **argument** verified at the call site only.
- `BusinessSearchCatalogRetrievalTests`, `BusinessSearchQuestionRedactionEndpointTests`, `BusinessSearchRangeQueryIntegrationTests`, `KnowledgeAudienceDtoValidationTests`, `BusinessSearchMoneyToolTests`, the web/mobile suites — not read (named in SKILL only).
- `AIAssistant:RateLimiting:Enabled` per environment: if false, `AiRateLimitingService.cs:45-48` makes every burst limit inert (config, not code).
- Model compliance with the prompt rules (T13 answer level) — not verifiable statically.
- Team/Branch scope arms (`ResourceScopeEvaluator.cs:48-68`, `WorkListNarrowing.cs:53-55`) are granted by no role today; AG2-02 is theoretical until one is.
- Mobile screen remount on workspace switch (the hook aborts on `businessId` change; the screen was not read).
- `es/fr/hi/gu.json` wording of the refusal keys (the convention test pins presence, not content).
- `KnowledgeController.cs:995-1040` (audience DTO projection) — not read.
### 18.3 Cost, performance, RU, leaks, thread safety (AG4)

### AG4 — Cost, performance, RU, leaks, thread safety, duplicate client calls — Business Search / Ask Clinket

Scope read end-to-end: all 14 `Services/BusinessSearch/*.cs` + 17 `Tools/*.cs`, `BusinessSearchController.cs`, `BusinessSearchUsageCounterRepository.cs`, `McpSessionService.cs`, `AiSessionRepository.cs`, `AiRateLimitingService.cs`, `PlatformLimitAlerts.cs`, `ApiCatalogIsolationAlarm.cs`, `ProviderKnowledgeSearchService(.Provider).cs`, `ProviderCatalogSearchService.cs`, `FullProviderContextService.cs`, `KnowledgeContentArtifactStore.cs`, `CosmosDbRepository.cs`, `CosmosContainerPolicies.cs` (all), the repositories each tool calls, Program.cs registrations, appsettings `BusinessSearch`/`RateLimiting`/`VoiceCatalog`, and every web/mobile file named in the brief plus the hooks/services they call. Measured RU figures are quoted only from `AUDIT-P4.7 §4`, `AUDIT-P4.6 §2`, `MEASUREMENTS-P4.5`; everything else is marked unmeasured.

#### 1. Findings table

| ID | Sev | Class | Where | Defect (concrete) | Root fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AG4-01 | **Medium** | LEAK (unbounded singleton growth) | `AiRateLimitingService.cs:235,246-248`; caller `BusinessSearchController.cs:314-321` | Feedback bucket key = `business-search-feedback:{membershipId}:{businessId}`; reaper does `kvp.Key.Split(':')[0]` → `"business-search-feedback"` → `GetEndpointConfig` (224-232) returns null → `continue`. One `RateLimitBucket` per (member,business) that ever rated, never removed, in a singleton (`Program.cs:857`). Same hole for any custom-endpoint caller whose prefix isn't in the fixed switch | Store `WindowMinutes` on the bucket at creation and reap from it; never re-derive config from a key prefix |
| AG4-02 | **Medium** | COST — duplicate scans on the catalogue miss path | `ProviderCatalogSearchService.cs:357-358,174,510-525,566-567`; `SearchServicesTool.cs:129-141`; `appsettings.json:164` (`ConfirmEmptyAgainstCosmos: true`) | Index leg finds 0 → returns null → line 174 runs `LookupViaCosmosAsync` with `IncludeUnapproved=true, UnapprovedOnly=false` = FULL CONTAINS scan (48.22 RU measured). The concurrent Cosmos leg (`UnapprovedOnly=true`, 36.07 RU today / ~3 RU once `/approvalStatus/?` is applied) is a strict subset of it. Both legs returning 0 each call `NearestGroupsAsync` → `GetPublicCatalogGroupCountsAsync` GROUP BY (`ServiceRepository.cs:222`), a business-level result computed **twice per rendering**. 3 alphabets × miss = 6 CONTAINS scans + 6 identical GROUP BYs for one "nothing found" | (a) Make the confirm-empty fallback approved-only (`c.approvalStatus = @approved`, index-served) so the two scans partition instead of overlap; (b) memoize `NearestGroupsAsync(businessId)` per request (service is Scoped, `Program.cs:1042`) |
| AG4-03 | **Medium** | COST — full-partition read per call (P4.5 list_customers class, unnamed branch) | `ListCustomersTool.cs:73-74` → `BusinessCustomerRepository.cs:30-33` → `CosmosDbRepository.cs:648-653` | With no `nameContains` (which the tool description *invites*, `BusinessSearchToolCatalog.cs:53-55`) the WHOLE `BusinessCustomer` partition is read (`SELECT * WHERE type`, 100/page, "900 rows / nine round trips" per the P4.5 note) to keep 10 rows + a count (`Bound`, line 113) | `SELECT VALUE COUNT(1)` + `SELECT TOP 10 … ORDER BY c.createdAt DESC` (binds `(type, createdAt DESC)`, policy 97-101); for narrow scope, read only the ≤`_maxAssignedCustomerIds` reachable ids |

Cached per business (Size clamp ✓) but N concurrent cold callers build N times | A Business-Search projection (profile+areas+availability+offers+rating = 5 reads) or single-flight the shared build |
| AG4-06 | Low | DUPLICATE — session read twice per ask | `BusinessSearchController.cs:133` + `McpSessionService.cs:62` | Happy path = 2 point reads + 1 replace; the first read's ETag is discarded | Carry the ETag into the first CAS attempt; re-read only on `Conflict` |

SKILL §11.2 "invalidated on content change" is false; content is written by the Functions host, which cannot reach this in-process cache. Alphabet set stale ≤30 min (`Scripts:CacheMinutes`) after a first non-Latin upload → one leg short | Delete the dead member (§22.2) or drive expiry from a content version the API can read |
