#### C. Leaks and disposal — CLEAN, with the residuals named

- Model client (`BusinessSearchModelClient.cs:80-120`): `using lease/request/timeout/response`, `await using stream`, `using reader`, per-frame `using JsonDocument`+`Clone()` — all inside the async iterator; consumer is `await foreach` (`Agent:201`) and the outer `finally` (`Agent:86-90`) cancels `linked` and awaits the producer with `SuppressThrowing`, so early disposal runs the iterator's finallys ✓. `HttpClient` from `IHttpClientFactory` named client, Scoped service (`Program.cs:1053-1059`) — handler rotation intact ✓; no `new HttpClient(` in `clinqetinfrastructure/Services` ✓.
- Controller: `await using var frames` (140-142); in-flight `MoveNextAsync` awaited before dispose (177-178) — **P1 H2 still handled** ✓; `using var pingWindow` per iteration ✓; `PersistTurnAsync` on `CancellationToken.None` by design.
- Agent: `using linked` (79), `using budget` (137), `writer.Complete()` in `finally` (364); bounded channel 100/`Wait` — an abandoned reader blocks the producer only until `linked.Cancel()` in the consumer `finally` ✓.
- Detached work: alphabet lookup continues past the 250 ms caller budget, bounded by `LookupTimeoutMs × 20` = 5 s ceiling CTS (`using`, 130-131); `Lazy<Task>` removed in `finally` after the cache write (120-125); `FacetAsync` catches everything so the detached task never faults ✓.
- Fire-and-forget: `ApiCatalogIsolationAlarm.cs:52,65` (`_ = PublishLeakAsync` / `SafeAsync`, both try/catch) ✓; `AiRateLimitingService.cs:76,119` `_ = ReportCapacityPressureAsync` inside a `lock` — `PublishAsync` is `async Task` with try/catch (`PlatformLimitAlerts.cs:212-247`) ✓ (only `TryClaim` sits outside the try).
- `FeedIterator` `using` at `CosmosDbRepository.cs:429,548,607,678` and `KnowledgeDocumentRepository.cs:126,322,350` ✓; blob/gzip streams `await using` (`KnowledgeContentArtifactStore.cs:102-103`, `BusinessSearchDocumentService.cs:67-68`) ✓; `await using var db` (`BookingDisputeService.cs:441`) ✓; rate-limiter `Timer` disposed (268-273) ✓.
- `Task.Run`: only `CosmosDbRepository.BulkInsertAsync` (off-path). `lock` over `await`: none. Static mutable state: none beyond thread-safe collections / `static readonly JsonSerializerOptions`.
- Clients: web `askOnce` disarms the stall timer and removes the abort listener in `finally` (137-140) ✓; mobile `streamSse` guards every handler on `settled` and clears the timer (90-181) ✓ — `xhr.responseText` re-read per progress is O(n²) bytes over the bridge (platform-inherent, documented in the file).

#### D. Thread safety — CLEAN, with notes

- `BusinessSearchCitationRegistry`: plain `Dictionary`s (`clinqetcore/…/BusinessSearchCitationRegistry.cs:16-18`), `new`-ed per answer (`Agent:101`), mutated only from the sequential `RunToolsAsync` loop (412-414); `SearchServicesTool` touches it only after `Task.WhenAll(lookups)` (144-145) ✓. Not shared.
- Alphabet single-flight: `Lazy<Task>(ExecutionAndPublication)` (100); removal after the cache `Set` (120-125) ✓. Residual: a caller that missed the cache before the `Set` and reaches `StartOrJoin` after `TryRemove` starts one extra bounded lookup — benign.
- Roster/tz/name-matcher caches: read-or-set, no read-modify-write ✓. `ProviderKnowledgeSearchService._visibilityCache` (Provider.cs:284) is an unsynchronized nullable tuple on a Scoped instance — safe because tool calls are sequential per request; would tear under concurrent use of one instance (note only).
- Usage counter: read-both → increment-and-compare (ctrl 404-434); ceiling exact; residuals = (a) the crossing request's own unit, (b) when the same member races itself at 59/60 the business unit at 424 is burned before the member refusal at 431-433, (c) limits are charged before `GetMemberSessionAsync`/the agent run, so a downstream 500 still spends the unit.
- `ProviderCatalogSearchService`: no mutable instance state under the union's concurrent legs ✓. `McpSessionService` CAS: 4 attempts, `Random.Shared` jitter, `Gone` terminal ✓. `AiRateLimitingService`: `ConcurrentDictionary` + per-bucket `lock`, double-checked `RefreshWindow` ✓. `AiBudgetGovernor.AcquireAsync` returns `_noop` for the Interactive lane (line 66) — no wait ✓.

#### E. Per-answer cost model (settings: iterations 4, calls/iteration 4, legs 3, `ToolResultMaxChars` 6 000, `MaxToolResultCharsPerAnswer` 18 000, `WorkLists.MaxRows` 10, `MaxImagesPerAnswer` 4)

**Typical 2-turn knowledge answer, Latin-only business, warm caches:** model calls 2 · embeddings 1 · search requests 1 (BM25+vector in ONE `SearchAsync`, Provider.cs:254) · Cosmos: #1-2 (~2 RU per the controller comment), #3-4 (2 PATCHes, unmeasured), #5-6 (2 RU), #7 (unmeasured), #9 (1 projection, unmeasured), #10 ≤k point reads (1 RU each) · blob HEADs = distinct non-FAQ docs (verified per DISTINCT doc, `SearchKnowledgeTool.cs:282-293`; but per CALL, AG4-09) · SAS ≤ 4 images × 2 (thumb+full) per answer (cap at `Agent:444-453` and tool 164-166) · SQL 0 for a member who cannot narrow by person; roster (cached 60 s) for an owner regardless of question (AG4-17) · alphabets: cache hit (warmed by `prepare`).

**Worst 4-iteration, 3 alphabets, mixed tools:** model calls 4 (4th without tools) · ≤12 tool calls · `search_knowledge`: 3 embeddings + 3 search requests per call, visibility once per request · `search_services`: per rendering 1-3 index requests (+1 embedding on miss, `VectorMode: OnEmptyResult`) + 1 unapproved scan (36→~3 RU) [+ on miss: 1 full scan 48 RU + 2 GROUP BYs] → hit path 104 RU/question measured; miss path ≈ 3×(48+36)+6×GROUP-BY (unmeasured) · work tools 2 indexed queries each; `invoice_totals` ≈ 3.78+5.45+overdue RU; `list_customers` whole partition; `inbox_summary` keyset page + whole-partition projection + 1 SQL; `get_insights` cached; `list_team_members` 5 SQL cmds; `search_refund_requests` 2 SQL · roster cold 6 SQL cmds; tz 1 point read (30 min); `voice_ctx` cold fan-out.

Per-iteration that could be per-answer: 17 tool schemas re-`Deserialize<JsonElement>`d per model call (`ModelClient:71`, ≤68 parses/answer, CPU only); `CheckTokenRateLimitAsync`/`RecordTokenUsageAsync` per iteration are in-memory ✓. Per-ask that could be per-business: the visibility sweep (#9) is per request — a business-level fact. Done twice: #5/#6, AG4-02 (scan + GROUP BY), AG4-09, AG4-10, #30 per call. **Verified:** audience sweep memoized per request (key `businessId|isOwnerOrAdmin|roles`, ReadFailed not cached) ✓; roster read once per turn via the 60 s cache (`IsSoloAsync`/`ListAsync`/`ResolveAsync` share `bsearch:roster:`) ✓; `IsSolo` lazy in `ResolveScopeAsync` (192-195) ✓; profile/availability/offers tools share ONE `voice_ctx` entry, Size-set ✓; `invoice_totals` reads no profile (currency via cached `CountryResolutionService`).

#### F. Client HTTP requests per user action

**Web** — (1) page open: `POST prepare` ×1 (`useBusinessSearch.js:69-72`, deps `[activeBusinessId]`), `GET business/members` ×1 if `team.read` (page 95-108), `GET knowledge` ×1 if `voice.read` (113-125), services ×0-1 via 60 s single-flight shared with the header (`useBusinessServices.js:34-64`), `localStorage` recent ×1; effect deps `[can, access, accessError, activeBusinessId]` are stable (`can` = `useCallback([access])`, `BusinessContext.jsx:326-329`; `access` set once per load) — no re-fire per render ✓. `reactStrictMode: true` (`next.config.mjs:3`) doubles these in **dev only**. (2) one ask: `POST ask` ×1 (+1 only on 401 with zero frames received, 159-165); analytics batched 2 s (`analyticsTracker.js:10,321`); no per-keystroke request (`AskBox.jsx:107` → `setQuestion`). (3) follow-up: identical, `sessionId` attached; no extra reads ✓. (4) reopen: `GET sessions/{id}` ×1 (303); never re-asks ✓. (5) source: `GET documents/{docId}/pages/{n}` or `/text` ×1 (`SourcePanel.jsx:33-61`, deps `[citation, action, page]`; Escape listener bound once via ref 66-72 ✓; whole-document control only when `hasFullText` ✓ `SourceCard.jsx:307`). (6) rating: helpful → analytics only; not helpful → `POST feedback/not-helpful` ×1, re-POSTed on repeated taps (AG4-15). (7) workspace switch: `contextEpoch` resets the hook (77-98), the layout remounts content, so prepare/members/knowledge fire once for the new business ✓. Polls: none. Unused results: AG4-13. Per-delta storage: fixed (recent keyed on `running`, 130-133) ✓; per-delta CPU: AG4-12.

**Mobile** — (1) screen open: `prepare` ×1 (`useBusinessSearch.ts:80`), `getKnowledge` ×1 (`voice.read`), `getMembers` ×1 (`team.read`), `listBusinessServiceNames` ×0-1 (60 s cache + single-flight, `businessSearchService.ts:212-238`), AsyncStorage recent ×1. (2) ask: XHR `POST ask` ×1 (+1 on 401), `getToken` + AsyncStorage `language` read per ask (65-74), `rememberQuestion` get+set once after the stream; analytics batched. (3) follow-up: same. (4) reopen: `GET sessions/{id}` ×1. (5) source: 1 GET (`SourceSheet.tsx:42-69`). (6) rating: as web (AG4-15). (7) switch: `[businessId, abort]` reset; reads re-fire once when `access` lands (effects gated by `capabilityReadState`). Per-delta AsyncStorage: fixed (116-121) ✓; `memberNames: roster` is state, so `ask` identity is stable per render ✓; `t` in the chips memo is stable ✓.

#### G. Server-side per-delta cost

Per delta: one bounded-channel write (`Agent:208-212`), one `JsonSerializer.Serialize(frame, SseJsonOptions)` + `WriteAsync` + `FlushAsync` (ctrl 494-507 — a flush per token, normal SSE), the ping CTS/timer churn (AG4-07). No per-delta logging (usage once per turn, `LogUsage` 655-661), no per-delta redaction, `StringBuilder` accumulation ✓. Model side per SSE line: `line[5..].Trim()` + `JsonDocument.Parse` + `Clone` (110-131) — necessary. `: ping` only while a frame is pending ≥15 s (153-160); channel capacity 100/`Wait` ✓. Serializer options: `RequestJson` (22-26), `ModelFacingJson` (`ToolBase:15-20`), `PayloadShape` (`ProviderKnowledgeSearchService.cs:33-36`) all `UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping` ✓; `SseJsonOptions` (ctrl 47-51) is client-facing on the default encoder — non-Latin deltas travel as `\uXXXX` (≈2× wire bytes for Hindi/Gujarati; functionally fine); the two model-facing exceptions are AG4-08.

#### Verified clean (how)
No cross-partition op (every call site traced to a PK argument; base repo throws without one). Every `IMemoryCache` write read on the path sets `Size`. `NeedsExactTotal` waived with an index (tool 123 / repo 271-279). Group B lists bind existing composites (table A). Citations emitted once (`emitted` set, `Agent:432-434`); images capped per answer. SSE enumerator disposal ordering intact. Alphabet lookup single-flighted and bounded. Fire-and-forget sites all observed. No `Task.Run`, no `new HttpClient()`, no lock over await on the path.

#### Not verified
`AzureStorageService.GetUrlForReadAsync` (in-process SAS vs user-delegation key call) and `DerivativeBlobExistsAsync`/`GetBlobSizeAsync` (assumed one HEAD each); `FullProviderContextService` fan-out repository query shapes and `CategoryRepository.GetCategoryByIdAsync`; `CountryResolutionService.ResolveByBusinessIdAsync` underlying read; `CallSummaryRepository.NeedsReviewFilter` SQL text; RU of the counter PATCHes, session replace, visibility projection and the GROUP BY in #14; prod composite binding for the `isDeleted`-filtered `updatedAt` sorts (#18) — shared with the Invoices page; `useDictation`/mic network behaviour (idle unless pressed); analytics immediate-flush thresholds (batching itself confirmed); mobile `apiClient` refresh/dedup internals; `ProviderContextCacheMinutes` value. No file was created or modified inside any repo; scratch directory `…\scratchpad\AG4` holds nothing.

### 18.5 Data safety, idempotency, concurrency (AG7)

#### AG7 — DATA SAFETY, IDEMPOTENCY, CONCURRENCY — Business Search / Ask Clinket

Read end to end: `McpSessionService`, `AiSession`, `AiSessionRepository`, `BusinessSearchController`, `BusinessSearchAgent`, `BusinessSearchModelClient`, `AiBudgetGovernor`, `AiRateLimitingService`, `AiUsageCounter` + both counter repositories, `BusinessAlphabetService`, `KnowledgeManagementService` (all 1196 lines), `KnowledgeDocumentRepository`, `KnowledgeDocument`, `KnowledgeMetadataMergeService`, `KnowledgeIngestQueue`, `KnowledgeIngestProcessorFunction` (handler, `ProcessAsync`, merge/commit helpers), `AdminAlertRepository` + `CosmosDbRepository` write paths, `CosmosContainerPolicies`, `CosmosRetryPolicyFactory`, API `Program.cs` Cosmos client, `KnowledgeController` audience action, both client hooks, `AnalyticsProcessorFunction`, `UserInteractionAnalyticsService`, `events.json` queue definitions, and the pinning tests named below.

##### 1. Findings

| # | Sev | Scope | Finding (concrete interleaving) | CLASS | ROOT fix | §0.7 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **D1** | **Medium** | 1 Sessions | `[[partial-answer]]` is appended **before** the `SessionTurnMaxChars` cut, so it is the part that gets cut. (1) Model answers ~5,000 chars with `finish_reason=length` → `partial=true`. (2) `BusinessSearchController.cs:464` builds `answer + PartialAnswerMarker`. (3) `McpSessionService.cs:128` stores `content[..4000]` — marker gone. (4) Replay: client `stripPartialMarker` finds nothing → renders as complete; the next follow-up replays a mid-sentence answer to the model as finished. Same for any non-partial answer > 4,000 chars (silent cut, no marker). At `MaxCompletionTokens` 1500 an English table answer is ~4.5–6k chars, so this is the common long-answer case, not an edge. The only pin, `BusinessSearchControllerIntegrationTests.cs:878-905`, uses a 54-char answer. | Ordering of "mark" vs "cap" split across two files | Cap to `SessionTurnMaxChars − marker.Length` **then** append; treat a cap-cut as partial. Put both in `McpSessionService.AppendTo` (pass `partial`), delete the controller's marker concat; add a >4000-char partial case to the integration test | No |

No finding requires a schema change; §0.7 is not engaged by any fix above.

##### 2. Details and answers to the brief

**Sessions (scope 1).** CAS is exactly as described: `GetSessionWithETagAsync` (Polly on the **read** only, `AiSessionRepository.cs:37`) → mutate → `TryReplaceSessionAsync(ifMatch)` → `Replaced|Conflict|Gone` (94-122). Bound `MaxCasAttempts = 4`, jitter `Random.Shared.Next(10, 40)` only between attempts (`McpSessionService.cs:19,101-102`); `Gone` returns immediately (91-97); the create branch on 409 `continue`s without delay (69), which is fine because the re-read then finds the winner. Cosmos client options (`Program.cs:433-440`) tune only `MaxRetryAttemptsOnRateLimitedRequests`/`MaxRetryWaitTimeOnRateLimitedRequests` — 429 replays are safe (the request was rejected, not executed). There is **no `metadata["rev"]`** anywhere; the ETag is the sole version and nothing else is stamped — the brief's item does not exist in code. Membership: stamped at `157`, required at `111-114` (unstamped legacy rows are nobody's). Trim: `Math.Max(2, FollowUpTurns*2)` keeping the newest (135-140). TTL: `AiSession.Ttl` is `[JsonProperty("ttl")]` (`AiSession.cs:43-44`) — the real Cosmos TTL — honoured because Communications has `DefaultTimeToLive = -1` (`CosmosContainerPolicies.cs:571`); it is re-set on every replace (84), so it slides 7 days from the **last** turn. Two follow-ups in flight: A and B both read history H; whichever persists second gets a 412, re-reads H+other, appends — neither lost, neither doubled (no replay path exists application-side). Crash between answer and persist: the counters were incremented before streaming (`Controller:424,431`), nothing is stored; on reopen the member sees the previous exchange, or `ConversationGone` if it was the first. An Error-only stream stores nothing (457) — question lost, unit spent (residual). Stop-then-ask: the aborted request persists on `CancellationToken.None` (471) **after** the new ask may already have read the session, so the stopped exchange can land **after** the newer one in `History`; nothing lost, order can invert, and replay shows the last stored turn (residual, Low).

**Counters (scope 2).** Read both → refuse → increment → compare (`Controller:397-436`); atomic `PatchOperation.Increment` with create-on-404 and 409→patch (`BusinessSearchUsageCounterRepository.cs:67-118`); ids `aiusage_{biz}_bsearch_{yyyyMMdd}` / `aiusage_{biz}_bsearchm_{membershipId}_{yyyyMMdd}`, pk businessId (120-126). TTL is **not** "end of day": it is `UsageCounterTtlSeconds` = 259200 (3 d), set at create (93), sliding from the last PATCH's `_ts`; the date-keyed id is what rolls the day, TTL only garbage-collects, and SystemData `DefaultTimeToLive = -1` (692) makes it effective — correct. A Cosmos failure on either read propagates uncaught → global middleware 500: **fail closed**, no spend — right direction, generic copy. The "one unit once" residual has a second face: a business unit is burned when the member increment refuses or throws after it. In-process buckets: singleton per process, so N instances ⇒ N×6/min burst; the durable caps stay exact — accepted design, state it in the skill.

**Audience write (scope 3).** `SetSearchAudienceAsync` (`KMS:867-922`) validates every key against the catalogue **before** any read (881-892, unknown key → `UnknownRoleKey` → 400 at `KnowledgeController.cs:603-605`), trims/dedups, the DTO caps at 32 (`KnowledgeDtos.cs:116`; the 64-char per-key cap is effectively enforced by `IsKnownRoleKey`, the DTO comment is stale), `Deleting` refused (904), `Processing` allowed (898-903), server no-op compare (906-909), Team nulls the list (914), CAS ×3 → `Conflict` → **409** (606-608). Two devices: both read etag e1; the second gets 412, re-reads, compares, writes its own distinct value — last distinct writer wins, no unrelated field is ever clobbered. Ingest commit: `CommitAsync` (2548-2560) re-reads inside its own CAS and the Ready mutation (966-1000) touches only status/hash/title/pages/passages/analytics/images — an audience written during Processing survives.

**Duplicate merge (scope 4).** `ApplyProviderChoices` (2507-2528) never widens: `Roles` only, `false` only, non-empty links only, type ≠ `Other`. Re-read immediately before choices (2429). A merge that cannot commit throws (2445-2449) before any teardown; the outer `Run` (300-373) abandons on non-final deliveries and on the final one marks Failed + provider notice + admin alert + DLQ; `maxDeliveryCount` 5 (`events.json:1170`, Functions `appsettings.json:1000`). `MetadataOnly` requeue id `{biz}:{docId}:{mode}:{updatedAt ticks}` (`KnowledgeIngestQueue.cs:26-27`) on a queue with duplicate detection PT10M + sessions (`events.json:1164-1169`). Redelivery after a committed merge re-applies the same values (idempotent) and, because `twin.UpdatedAt` moved, mints a new `MetadataOnly` id → one extra re-cut (accepted residual #10). `ClearedFailureReason` at all four forward-flipping commits: 548, 774, 969, 2537; `FailAsync:2678` deliberately overwrites with the Error_ key. Survivor keeps docId/pk/cards; incoming cards/images/row/artifact/blob go only after `merged` (2466-2488).

**Not-helpful (scope 5).** `AlertId = bsfb_{answerId}_{membershipId}` (338); 409 → `InvalidOperationException` (`CosmosDbRepository.cs:230`) → caught → 200; `IsMintedId` = `Guid.TryParseExact(…, "N")` (481-483); Answer capped 8000, Question 500 (`BusinessSearchDtos.cs:30,34`); TTL `FeedbackAlertTtlDays*86400` (349); pk = month (339, re-stamped from `CreatedAt` at `AdminAlertRepository.cs:43`) and the create is partition-scoped (45 → `new PartitionKey` at 220); Severity Low (344); metadata is ids/counts/language only, Description is the member's own question and answer (348) — the question is **not** name-redacted here, unlike analytics (admin-only, owner-approved). Note `AddItemAsync:215` re-stamps `CreatedAt` **after** the pk month was computed — a sub-millisecond month-boundary skew, platform-wide, pre-existing. Same-answer taps in two different months would create two rows (different pk) — unreachable in practice since a replay carries no id.

**Alphabet cache (scope 6).** Single-flight `Lazy<Task>` with `ExecutionAndPublication` (35, 97-100); any `Resolved` result including empty is cached, `Unresolved` is not (107-117); `_inFlight` entry removed **after** the cache write (120-125). Invalidation: none exists (D2).

**Knowledge writes from the API (scope 7).** Every write in this feature is `TryReplaceAsync(row, row.ETag!)`: confirm 188/251(create), delete-mark 403, reprocess 434, details 788, sharing 859, audience 916, image 956/1019, FAQ 672/730/1108. No blind upsert is reachable (D6 is the uncalled method).

**`/approvalStatus/?` (scope 8).** Present in the policy (`CosmosContainerPolicies.cs:42`). `ServiceRepository.BuildCatalogPredicate` (303-312) adds `c.approvalStatus != @approved` unconditionally and its own comment says the index "is NOT assumed"; `SearchServicesTool` gates on `IndexAvailable` only (the search index, not Cosmos). Correctness is identical with or without the path — only RU differs (measured 36.07 RU unindexed vs 11.13 narrowing by an indexed path). Cosmos index transformation is online: reads keep being served, the not-yet-indexed predicate is evaluated by loading documents (today's cost) until it completes. A stamp not yet re-run pays ~36 RU per rendering on the unapproved leg — cost only, no code assumes consistency on the path.

**Service Bus handlers (scope 9).** Ingest: the row is the work order (395); Ready idempotent exit (461-476); Delete idempotent (396-402); tombstone CAS before the final commit (935-947) and post-commit re-read (1024-1030). MetadataOnly: `ApplyAsync` (`KnowledgeMetadataMergeService.cs:90-139`) patches the same values on redelivery; a redelivery onto a Processing row finishes the re-cut (431-460). Analytics: `EventId` is server-minted per send (`UserInteractionAnalyticsService.cs:276`), the Service Bus message id is random (118 → `ServiceBusService.cs:166`) so `analytics-events` duplicate detection (`events.json:189`) is inert for this producer; the processor abandons a whole group on any failure after possibly having written earlier day-buckets (`AnalyticsProcessorFunction.cs:230-234, 291-293`) → duplicates in raw files, folded at nightly compaction by EventId (`AnalyticsCompactionFunction.cs:32,359`). At-least-once, eventually deduped — pre-existing pipeline behaviour, not introduced by this programme; a client-side POST retry is a genuine duplicate.

**Streaming concurrency (scope 10).** Interactive lane holds **no** admission lease (`AiBudgetGovernor.cs:66` returns `_noop`), so cancellation has nothing to free; the client abort rides `HttpContext.RequestAborted` (128) into the agent's linked CTS (79) and tears down the model stream. Two asks from one member: allowed, both pay burst + both daily increments, both stream, both persist under CAS. Both hooks abort the prior run and bump `runIdRef` (web 125-132, mobile 143-150), so the loser's continuation is inert. Prepare vs ask: same `Lazy`; `GetAsync` waits ≤ `LookupTimeoutMs` (250 ms) then proceeds `Unresolved` (uncached) while the warm publishes (55-79).

##### 4. Not verified

- Cosmos .NET SDK Direct-mode internal replay of a write whose response was lost (SDK internals). Application code sets only the two 429 options (`Program.cs:437-438`) and no custom handlers; any post-commit replay would double a session turn via the CAS re-read — but I found no application-level path that produces one.
- Cosmos online index-transformation semantics for a newly included path (documented platform behaviour, not observable in code), and whether any stamp has `/approvalStatus/?` applied today (owner action).
- `AiRateLimitingService` has no test suite at all, so D3 is unpinned in both directions.
- No test drives two concurrent `AppendTurnsAsync` writers against the real emulator; the CAS loop is pinned only by a single scripted Conflict (`_OnConflict_RereadsAndRetries`).
- Minor convention drift, not audited further: `McpSessionService` hardcodes 4 attempts / 10–40 ms while `KnowledgeDocumentRepository.cs:39-41` binds the `CosmosDb:MaxConcurrencyRetries/ConcurrencyRetryBaseDelayMs/ConcurrencyRetryJitterMs` dials (§0.12).

No files were created, edited or deleted in any repo; nothing was written outside the AG7 scratchpad, which holds no artefacts.
